python3\n\"\"\"\nAC Optimal Power Flow Solver\nSolves the AC-OPF problem based on the mathematical model for finding\na least-cost, AC-feasible operating point.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nfrom casadi import *\nimport warnings\nwarnings.filterwarnings('ignore')\n\n\nclass ACOPFSolver:\n    def __init__(self, network_file):\n        \"\"\"Initialize the solver with network data.\"\"\"\n        with open(network_file, 'r') as f:\n            self.network = json.load(f)\n\n        self.base_mva = self.network['baseMVA']\n        self.parse_network()\n\n    def parse_network(self):\n        \"\"\"Parse network data from MATPOWER-like format.\"\"\"\n        # Parse buses\n        self.buses = {}\n        bus_data = self.network['bus']\n        for b in bus_data:\n            bus_id = int(b[0])\n            self.buses[bus_id] = {\n                'id': bus_id,\n                'type': int(b[1]),  # 1=load, 2=gen, 3=slack\n                'pd_mw': b[2],\n                'qd_mvar': b[3],\n                'vmin': b[11],\n                'vmax': b[12],\n                'base_kv': b[9]\n            }\n\n        # Parse generators\n        self.gens = {}\n        gen_data = self.network['gen']\n        gencost_data = self.network['gencost']\n        for i, g in enumerate(gen_data):\n            gen_id = i + 1\n            bus_id = int(g[0])\n            self.gens[gen_id] = {\n                'id': gen_id,\n                'bus': bus_id,\n                'pg_mw': g[1],\n                'qg_mvar': g[2],\n                'qmax_mvar': g[3],\n                'qmin_mvar': g[4],\n                'vg': g[5],\n                'pmax_mw': g[8],\n                'pmin_mw': g[9]\n            }\n\n            # Add cost coefficients (quadratic cost model)\n            cost = gencost_data[i]\n            if int(cost[0]) == 2:  # polynomial cost\n                n = int(cost[1])\n                if n == 3:  # quadratic\n                    self.gens[gen_id]['c2'] = cost[4]\n                    self.gens[gen_id]['c1'] = cost[5]\n                    self.gens[gen_id]['c0'] = cost[6] if len(cost) > 6 else 0\n                elif n == 2:  # linear\n                    self.gens[gen_id]['c2'] = 0\n                    self.gens[gen_id]['c1'] = cost[5]\n                    self.gens[gen_id]['c0'] = cost[6] if len(cost) > 6 else 0\n                else:\n                    self.gens[gen_id]['c2'] = 0\n                    self.gens[gen_id]['c1'] = 0\n                    self.gens[gen_id]['c0'] = 0\n            else:\n                self.gens[gen_id]['c2'] = 0\n                self.gens[gen_id]['c1'] = 0\n                self.gens[gen_id]['c0'] = 0\n\n        # Parse branches\n        self.branches = {}\n        branch_data = self.network['branch']\n        for i, br in enumerate(branch_data):\n            branch_id = i + 1\n            fbus = int(br[0])\n            tbus = int(br[1])\n            r = br[2]\n            x = br[3]\n            b = br[4]\n            s_limit = br[5]\n\n            # Calculate admittance\n            z = complex(r, x)\n            if abs(z) > 1e-6:\n                y = 1.0 / z\n            else:\n                y = 0\n\n            self.branches[branch_id] = {\n                'id': branch_id,\n                'from_bus': fbus,\n                'to_bus': tbus,\n                'r': r,\n                'x': x,\n                'b_shunt': b,\n                'y_real': y.real,\n                'y_imag': y.imag,\n                's_limit_mva': s_limit if s_limit > 0 else 9999,\n                'tap_ratio': br[8] if br[8] != 0 else 1.0,\n                'phase_shift': br[9]\n            }\n\n        self.num_buses = len(self.buses)\n        self.num_gens = len(self.gens)\n        self.num_branches = len(self.branches)\n\n        # Identify reference bus\n        self.ref_bus = None\n        for bus_id, bus in self.buses.items():\n            if bus['type'] == 3:\n                self.ref_bus = bus_id\n                break\n        if self.ref_bus is None:\n            self.ref_bus = 1  # Default to bus 1\n\n    def solve(self):\n        \"\"\"Solve the AC-OPF problem.\"\"\"\n        print(\"Building AC-OPF model...\")\n\n        # Decision variables\n        # Voltage: voltage magnitude (per-unit) and angle (radians)\n        vm = SX.sym('vm', self.num_buses)  # voltage magnitude\n        va = SX.sym('va', self.num_buses)  # voltage angle\n\n        # Generator real power\n        pg = SX.sym('pg', self.num_gens)   # real power\n        qg = SX.sym('qg', self.num_gens)   # reactive power\n\n        # Collect variables\n        x = vertcat(vm, va, pg, qg)\n\n        # Bounds\n        lbx = []\n        ubx = []\n\n        # Voltage magnitude bounds\n        for i in range(1, self.num_buses + 1):\n            lbx.append(self.buses[i]['vmin'])\n            ubx.append(self.buses[i]['vmax'])\n\n        # Voltage angle bounds (except reference bus)\n        for i in range(1, self.num_buses + 1):\n            if i == self.ref_bus:\n                lbx.append(0)\n                ubx.append(0)\n            else:\n                lbx.append(-np.pi)\n                ubx.append(np.pi)\n\n        # Generator real power bounds\n        for i in range(1, self.num_gens + 1):\n            lbx.append(self.gens[i]['pmin_mw'] / self.base_mva)\n            ubx.append(self.gens[i]['pmax_mw'] / self.base_mva)\n\n        # Generator reactive power bounds\n        for i in range(1, self.num_gens + 1):\n            lbx.append(self.gens[i]['qmin_mvar'] / self.base_mva)\n            ubx.append(self.gens[i]['qmax_mvar'] / self.base_mva)\n\n        lbx = np.array(lbx)\n        ubx = np.array(ubx)\n\n        # Objective: minimize generation cost\n        cost = 0\n        for i in range(1, self.num_gens + 1):\n            c2 = self.gens[i]['c2']\n            c1 = self.gens[i]['c1']\n            c0 = self.gens[i]['c0']\n            p = pg[i-1] * self.base_mva\n            cost += c2 * p**2 + c1 * p + c0\n\n        # Constraints\n        g = []  # constraint expressions\n        lbg = []  # constraint lower bounds\n        ubg = []  # constraint upper bounds\n\n        # Power flow equations (nodal balance)\n        for bus_id in range(1, self.num_buses + 1):\n            bus_idx = bus_id - 1\n            v_mag = vm[bus_idx]\n            v_ang = va[bus_idx]\n\n            # Calculate P and Q injections at this bus\n            p_inj = 0\n            q_inj = 0\n\n            # Generator contributions\n            for gen_id in range(1, self.num_gens + 1):\n                if self.gens[gen_id]['bus'] == bus_id:\n                    p_inj += pg[gen_id-1]\n                    q_inj += qg[gen_id-1]\n\n            # Load contributions (in per-unit)\n            pd = self.buses[bus_id]['pd_mw'] / self.base_mva\n            qd = self.buses[bus_id]['qd_mvar'] / self.base_mva\n            p_inj -= pd\n            q_inj -= qd\n\n            # Branch flows\n            p_flow = 0\n            q_flow = 0\n\n            for branch_id in range(1, self.num_branches + 1):\n                br = self.branches[branch_id]\n                fbus = br['from_bus']\n                tbus = br['to_bus']\n\n                # Power flow from this bus\n                if fbus == bus_id:\n                    # Calculate power flow from bus i to bus j\n                    v_i = v_mag\n                    a_i = v_ang\n\n                    # Need to find voltage at other end\n                    j_idx = tbus - 1\n                    v_j = vm[j_idx]\n                    a_j = va[j_idx]\n\n                    # Admittance parameters\n                    g_ij = br['y_real']\n                    b_ij = br['y_imag']\n                    b_c = br['b_shunt'] / 2.0\n                    tau = br['tap_ratio']\n                    phi = br['phase_shift']\n\n                    # Transform tap and phase shift\n                    tau_complex = tau * cos(phi) + 1j * tau * sin(phi)\n\n                    # Voltage angle difference\n                    da = a_i - a_j\n\n                    # Power flow equations\n                    p_ij = g_ij * (v_i**2 / (tau**2)) - (g_ij * cos(da) + b_ij * sin(da)) * v_i * v_j / tau\n                    q_ij = -(b_ij + b_c) * (v_i**2 / (tau**2)) + (b_ij * cos(da) - g_ij * sin(da)) * v_i * v_j / tau\n\n                    p_flow -= p_ij\n                    q_flow -= q_ij\n\n                elif tbus == bus_id:\n                    # Power flow from bus j to bus i\n                    i_idx = fbus - 1\n                    v_i = vm[i_idx]\n                    a_i = va[i_idx]\n\n                    v_j = v_mag\n                    a_j = v_ang\n\n                    g_ij = br['y_real']\n                    b_ij = br['y_imag']\n                    b_c = br['b_shunt'] / 2.0\n                    tau = br['tap_ratio']\n                    phi = br['phase_shift']\n\n                    da = a_i - a_j\n\n                    # Power flow from i to j, so reverse for j perspective\n                    p_ji = g_ij * v_j**2 - (g_ij * cos(da) + b_ij * sin(da)) * v_i * v_j / tau\n                    q_ji = -(b_ij + b_c) * v_j**2 + (b_ij * cos(da) - g_ij * sin(da)) * v_i * v_j / tau\n\n                    p_flow -= p_ji\n                    q_flow -= q_ji\n\n            # Power balance constraints: P_inj - P_flow = 0, Q_inj - Q_flow = 0\n            g.append(p_inj - p_flow)\n            lbg.append(0)\n            ubg.append(0)\n\n            g.append(q_inj - q_flow)\n            lbg.append(0)\n            ubg.append(0)\n\n        # Branch MVA limit constraints\n        for branch_id in range(1, self.num_branches + 1):\n            br = self.branches[branch_id]\n            fbus = br['from_bus'] - 1\n            tbus = br['to_bus'] - 1\n\n            g_ij = br['y_real']\n            b_ij = br['y_imag']\n            b_c = br['b_shunt'] / 2.0\n            tau = br['tap_ratio']\n            phi = br['phase_shift']\n\n            da = va[fbus] - va[tbus]\n\n            v_i = vm[fbus]\n            v_j = vm[tbus]\n\n            # Power flow from i to j\n            p_ij = g_ij * (v_i**2 / (tau**2)) - (g_ij * cos(da) + b_ij * sin(da)) * v_i * v_j / tau\n            q_ij = -(b_ij + b_c) * (v_i**2 / (tau**2)) + (b_ij * cos(da) - g_ij * sin(da)) * v_i * v_j / tau\n\n            s_ij = sqrt(p_ij**2 + q_ij**2)\n            s_limit = br['s_limit_mva'] / self.base_mva\n\n            g.append(s_ij)\n            lbg.append(0)\n            ubg.append(s_limit)\n\n            # Power flow from j to i\n            p_ji = g_ij * v_j**2 - (g_ij * cos(da) + b_ij * sin(da)) * v_i * v_j / tau\n            q_ji = -(b_ij + b_c) * v_j**2 + (b_ij * cos(da) - g_ij * sin(da)) * v_i * v_j / tau\n\n            s_ji = sqrt(p_ji**2 + q_ji**2)\n\n            g.append(s_ji)\n            lbg.append(0)\n            ubg.append(s_limit)\n\n        # Create NLP\n        nlp = {'x': x, 'f': cost, 'g': vertcat(*g)}\n\n        # Solver options\n        opts = {\n            'ipopt': {\n                'max_iter': 3000,\n                'tol': 1e-6,\n                'acceptable_tol': 1e-4,\n                'print_level': 0,\n                'mu_strategy': 'adaptive'\n            },\n            'print_time': False\n        }\n\n        # Create solver\n        solver = nlpsol('solver', 'ipopt', nlp, opts)\n\n        # Initial guess\n        x0 = np.ones(len(x))\n        # Set initial voltages\n        for i in range(self.num_buses):\n            x0[i] = 1.0  # voltage magnitude\n        # Set initial angles to 0\n        for i in range(self.num_buses):\n            x0[self.num_buses + i] = 0\n        # Set initial generator outputs\n        for i in range(self.num_gens):\n            x0[2*self.num_buses + i] = 0.5  # midpoint of bounds\n            x0[2*self.num_buses + self.num_gens + i] = 0\n\n        print(\"Solving AC-OPF...\")\n        result = solver(x0=x0, lbx=lbx, ubx=ubx, lbg=lbg, ubg=ubg)\n\n        # Extract results\n        x_opt = np.array(result['x']).flatten()\n\n        self.vm_opt = x_opt[:self.num_buses]\n        self.va_opt = x_opt[self.num_buses:2*self.num_buses]\n        self.pg_opt = x_opt[2*self.num_buses:2*self.num_buses + self.num_gens]\n        self.qg_opt = x_opt[2*self.num_buses + self.num_gens:]\n\n        # Get solver status\n        stats = solver.stats()\n        self.solver_status = 'optimal' if stats['return_status'] == 'Solve_Succeeded' else stats['return_status']\n\n        return result\n\n    def generate_report(self):\n        \"\"\"Generate the report.json file.\"\"\"\n        print(\"Generating report...\")\n\n        # Calculate summary statistics\n        total_gen_p = np.sum(self.pg_opt) * self.base_mva\n        total_gen_q = np.sum(self.qg_opt) * self.base_mva\n\n        total_load_p = sum(bus['pd_mw'] for bus in self.buses.values())\n        total_load_q = sum(bus['qd_mvar'] for bus in self.buses.values())\n\n        total_losses_p = total_gen_p - total_load_p\n\n        # Calculate total cost\n        total_cost = 0\n        for i in range(1, self.num_gens + 1):\n            c2 = self.gens[i]['c2']\n            c1 = self.gens[i]['c1']\n            c0 = self.gens[i]['c0']\n            p = self.pg_opt[i-1] * self.base_mva\n            total_cost += c2 * p**2 + c1 * p + c0\n\n        # Build generators list\n        generators = []\n        for i in range(1, self.num_gens + 1):\n            gen = self.gens[i]\n            generators.append({\n                'id': gen['id'],\n                'bus': gen['bus'],\n                'pg_MW': round(self.pg_opt[i-1] * self.base_mva, 2),\n                'qg_MVAr': round(self.qg_opt[i-1] * self.base_mva, 2),\n                'pmin_MW': round(gen['pmin_mw'], 2),\n                'pmax_MW': round(gen['pmax_mw'], 2),\n data.\"\"\"\n        with open(network_file, 'r') as f:\n            self.network = json.load(f)\n\n        self.base_mva = self.network['baseMVA']\n        self.parse_network()\n\n    def parse_network(self):\n        \"\"\"Parse network data from MATPOWER-like format.\"\"\"\n        # Parse buses\n        self.buses = {}\n        bus_data = self.network['bus']\n        for b in bus_data:\n            bus_id = int(b[0])\n            self.buses[bus_id] = {\n                'id': bus_id,\n                'type': int(b[1]),  # 1=load, 2=gen, 3=slack\n                'pd_mw': b[2],\n                'qd_mvar': b[3],\n                'vmin': b[11],\n                'vmax': b[12],\n                'base_kv': b[9]\n            }\n\n        # Parse generators\n        self.gens = {}\n        gen_data = self.network['gen']\n        gencost_data = self.network['gencost']\n        for i, g in enumerate(gen_data):\n            gen_id = i + 1\n            bus_id = int(g[0])\n           'qmin_mvar': g[4],\n                'vg': g[5],\n                'pmax_mw': g[8],\n                'pmin_mw': g[9]\n            }\n\n            # Add cost coefficients (quadratic cost model)\n            cost = gencost_data[i]\n            if int(cost[0]) == 2:  # polynomial cost\n                n = int(cost[1])\n                if n == 3:  # quadratic\n                    self.gens[gen_id]['c2'] = cost[4]\n                    self.gens[gen_id]['c1'] = cost[5]\n                    self.gens[gen_id]['c0'] = cost[6] if len(cost) > 6 else 0\n                elif n == 2:  # linear\n                    self.gens[gen_id]['c2'] = 0\n                    self.gens[gen_id]['c1'] = cost[5]\n                    self.gens[gen_id]['c0'] = cost[6] if len(cost) > 6 else 0\n                else:\n                    self.gens[gen_id]['c2'] = 0\n                    self.gens[gen_id]['c1'] = 0\n                    self.gens[gen_id]['c0'] = 0\n            else:\n                self.gens[gen_id]['c2'] = 0\n                self.gens[gen_id]['c1'] = 0\n                self.gens[gen_id]['c0'] = 0\n\n        # Parse branches\n        self.branches = {}\n        branch_data =  else:\n                y = 0\n\n            self.branches[branch_id] = {\n                'id': branch_id,\n                'from_bus': fbus,\n                'to_bus': tbus,\n                'r': self.num_branches = len(self.branches)\n\n        # Identify reference bus\n        self.ref_bus = None\n        for bus_id, bus in self.buses.items():\n            if bus['type'] == 3:\n problem.\"\"\"\n        print(\"Building AC-OPF model...\")\n\n        # Decision variables\n        # Voltage: voltage magnitude (per-unit) and angle (radians)\n        vm = SX.sym('vm', self.num_buses)  # voltage magnitude\n        va = SX.sym('va', self.num_buses)  # voltage angle\n\n        # Generator real power\n        pg = SX.sym('pg', self.num_gens)   # real power\n        qg = SX.sym('qg', self.num_gens)   # reactive power\n\n        # Collect variables\n        x = vertcat(vm, va, pg, qg)\n\n        # Bounds\n        lbx = []\n        ubx = []\n\n        # Voltage magnitude bounds\n        for i in range(1, self.num_buses + 1):\n            lbx.append(self.buses[i]['vmin'])\n            ubx.append(self.buses[i]['vmax'])\n\n        # Voltage angle bounds (except reference bus)\n        for i in range(1, self.num_buses + 1):\n            if i == self.ref_bus:\n                lbx.append(0)\n                ubx.append(0)\n            else:\n                lbx.append(-np.pi)\n                ubx.append(np.pi)\n\n        # Generator real power bounds\n        for i in range(1, self.num_gens + 1):\n            lbx.append(self.gens[i]['pmin_mw'] / self.base_mva)\n            ubx.append(self.gens[i]['pmax_mw'] / self.base_mva)\n\n        # Generator reactive power bounds\n        for i in range(1, self.num_gens + 1):\n            lbx.append(self.gens[i]['qmin_mvar'] / self.base_mva)\n            ubx.append(self.gens[i]['qmax_mvar'] / self.base_mva)\n\n        lbx = np.array(lbx)\n        ubx = np.array(ubx)\n\n        # Objective: minimize generation cost\n        cost = 0\n        for i in range(1, self.num_gens + 1):\n            c2 = self.gens[i]['c2']\n            c1 = self.gens[i]['c1']\n            c0 = self.gens[i]['c0']\n            p = pg[i-1] * self.base_mva\n            cost += c2 * p**2 + c1 * p + c0\n\n        # Constraints\n        g = []  # constraint expressions\n        lbg = []  # constraint lower bounds\n        ubg = []  # constraint upper bounds\n\n        # Power flow equations (nodal balance)\n        for bus_id in range(1, self.num_buses + 1):\n            bus_idx = bus_id - 1\n            v_mag = vm[bus_idx]\n            v_ang = va[bus_idx]\n\n            # Calculate P and Q injections at this bus\n            p_inj = 0\n            q_inj = 0\n\n            #           p_inj -= pd\n            q_inj -= qd\n\n            # Branch flows\n            p_flow = 0\n            q_flow = 0\n\n            for branch_id in range(1, self.num_branches + 1):\n                br = self.branches[branch_id]\n                fbus = br['from_bus']\n                tbus = br['to_bus']\n\n                # Power flow from this bus\n                if fbus == bus_id:\n                    # Calculate power flow from bus i to bus j\n                    v_i = v_mag\n                    a_i = v_ang\n\n                    # Need to find voltage at other end\n                    j_idx = tbus - 1\n                    v_j = vm[j_idx]\n                    a_j = va[j_idx]\n\n                    # Admittance parameters\n                    g_ij = br['y_real']\n                    b_ij = br['y_imag']\n                    b_c = br['b_shunt'] / 2.0\n                    tau = br['tap_ratio']\n                    phi = br['phase_shift']\n\n                    # Transform tap and phase shift\n                    tau_complex = tau * cos(phi) + 1j * tau * sin(phi)\n\n                    # Voltage angle difference\n                    da = a_i - a_j\n\n                    # Power flow equations\n                    p_ij = g_ij * (v_i**2 / (tau**2)) - (g_ij * cos(da) + b_ij * sin(da)) * v_i * v_j / tau\n                    q_ij = -(b_ij + b_c) * (v_i**2 / (tau**2)) + (b_ij * cos(da) - g_ij * sin(da)) * v_i * v_j / tau\n\n                    p_flow -= p_ij\n                    q_flow -= q_ij\n\n                elif tbus == bus_id:\n                    # Power flow from bus j to bus i\n                    i_idx = fbus - 1\n                    v_i = vm[i_idx]\n                    a_i = va[i_idx]\n\n                    v_j = v_mag\n                    a_j = v_ang\n\n                    g_ij = br['y_real']\n                    b_ij = br['y_imag']\n                    b_c = br['b_shunt'] / 2.0\n                    tau = br['tap_ratio']\n                    phi = br['phase_shift']\n\n                    da = a_i - a_j\n\n                    # Power flow from i to j, so reverse for j perspective\n                    p_ji = g_ij * v_j**2 - (g_ij * cos(da) + b_ij * sin(da)) * v_i * v_j / tau\n                    q_ji = -(b_ij + b_c) * v_j**2 + (b_ij * cos(da) - g_ij * sin(da)) * v_i * v_j / tau\n\n                    p_flow -= p_ji\n                    q_flow -= q_ji\n\n            # Power balance constraints: P_inj - P_flow = 0, Q_inj - Q_flow = 0\n            g.append(p_inj - p_flow)\n            lbg.append(0)\n            ubg.append(0)\n\n            g.append(q_inj - q_flow)\n            lbg.append(0)\n            ubg.append(0)\n\n        # Branch MVA limit constraints\n        for branch_id in range(1, self.num_branches + 1):\n            br = self.branches[branch_id]\n            fbus = br['from_bus'] - 1\n            tbus = br['to_bus'] - 1\n\n            g_ij = br['y_real']\n            b_ij = br['y_imag']\n            b_c = br['b_shunt'] / 2.0\n            tau = br['tap_ratio']\n            phi = br['phase_shift']\n\n            da = va[fbus] - va[tbus]\n\n            v_i = vm[fbus]\n            v_j = vm[tbus]\n\n            # Power flow from i to j\n            p_ij = g_ij * (v_i**2 / (tau**2)) - (g_ij * cos(da) + b_ij * sin(da)) * v_i * v_j / tau\n            q_ij = -(b_ij + b_c) * (v_i**2 / (tau**2)) + (b_ij * cos(da) - g_ij * sin(da)) * v_i * v_j / tau\n\n            s_ij = sqrt(p_ij**2 + q_ij**2)\n            s_limit = br['s_limit_mva'] / self.base_mva\n\n            g.append(s_ij)\n            lbg.append(0)\n            ubg.append(s_limit)\n\n            # Power flow from j to i\n            p_ji = g_ij * v_j**2 - (g_ij * cos(da) + b_ij * sin(da)) * v_i * v_j / tau\n            q_ji = -(b_ij + b_c) * v_j**2 + (b_ij * cos(da) - g_ij * sin(da)) * v_i * v_j / tau\n\n            s_ji = sqrt(p_ji**2 + q_ji**2)\n\n            g.append(s_ji)\n            lbg.append(0)\n            ubg.append(s_limit)\n\n        # Create NLP\n        nlp = {'x': x, 'f': cost, 'g': vertcat(*g)}\n\n        # Solver options\n        opts = {\n            'ipopt': {\n                'max_iter': 3000,\n                'tol': 1e-6,\n                'acceptable_tol': 1e-4,\n                'print_level': 0,\n                'mu_strategy': 'adaptive'\n            },\n            'print_time': False\n        }\n\n        # Create solver\n        solver = nlpsol('solver', 'ipopt', nlp, opts)\n\n        # Initial guess\n        x0 = np.ones(len(x))\n        # Set initial voltages\n        for i in range(self.num_buses):\n            x0[i] = 1.0  # voltage magnitude\n        # Set initial angles to 0\n        for i in range(self.num_buses):\n            x0[self.num_buses + i] = 0\n        #     print(\"Solving AC-OPF...\")\n        result = solver(x0=x0, lbx=lbx, ubx=ubx, lbg=lbg, ubg=ubg)\n\n        # Extract results\n        x_opt = np.array(result['x']).flatten()\n\n        self.vm_opt = x_opt[:self.num_buses]\n        self.va_opt = x_opt[self.num_buses:2*self.num_buses]\n        self.pg_opt = x_opt[2*self.num_buses:2*self.num_buses + self.num_gens]\n        self.qg_opt = x_opt[2*self.num_buses + self.num_gens:]\n\n        # Get solver status\n        stats = solver.stats()\n        self.solver_status = 'optimal' if stats['return_status'] == 'Solve_Succeeded' else stats['return_status']\n\n        return result\n\n    def generate_report(self):\n        \"\"\"Generate the report.json file.\"\"\"\n        print(\"Generating report...\")\n\n        # Calculate summary statistics\n        total_gen_p = np.sum(self.pg_opt) * self.base_mva\n        total_gen_q = np.sum(self.qg_opt) * self.base_mva\n\n        total_load_p = sum(bus['pd_mw'] for bus in self.buses.values())\n        total_load_q = sum(bus['qd_mvar'] for bus in self.buses.values())\n\n        total_losses_p = total_gen_p - total_load_p\n\n        # Calculate total cost\n        total_cost = 0\n        for i in range(1, self.num_gens + 1):\n            c2 = self.gens[i]['c2']\n            c1 = self.gens[i]['c1']\n            c0 = self.gens[i]['c0']\n            p =            'qg_MVAr': round(self.qg_opt[i-1] * self.base_mva, 2),\n                'pmin_MW': round(gen['pmin_mw'], 2),\n                'pmax_MW': round(gen['pmax_mw'], 2),\n                'qmin_MVAr': round(gen['qmin_mvar'], 2),\n                'qmax_MVAr': round(gen['qmax_mvar'], 2)\n            })\n\n        # Build buses list\n        buses = []\n        for i in range(1, self.num_buses + 1):\n            bus = self.buses[i]\n            buses.append({\n                'id': bus['id'],\n                'vm_pu': round(self.vm_opt[i-1], 4),\n                'va_deg': round(np.degrees(self.va_opt[i-1]), 2),\n                'vmin_pu': round(bus['vmin'], 3),\n                'vmax_pu': round(bus['vmax'], 3)\n            })\n\n        # Find most loaded branches (top 5)\n        branch_loadings = []\n        for i in range(1, self.num_branches + 1):\n            br = self.branches[i]\n            fbus = br['from_bus'] - 1\n            tbus = br['to_bus'] - 1\n\n            g_ij = br['y_real']\n            b_ij = br['y_imag']\n            b_c = br['b_shunt'] / 2.0\n            tau = br['tap_ratio']\n            phi = br['phase_shift']\n\n            da = self.va_opt[fbus] - self.va_opt[tbus]\n            v_i = self.vm_opt[fbus]\n            v_j = self.vm_opt[tbus]\n\n            # Power flow from i to j\n            p_ij = g_ij * (v_i**2 / (tau**2)) - (g_ij * np.cos(da) + b_ij * np.sin(da)) * v_i * v_j / tau\n            q_ij = -(b_ij + b_c) * (v_i**2 / (tau**2)) + (b_ij * np.cos(da) - g_ij * np.sin(da)) * v_i * v_j / tau\n            s_ij = np.sqrt(p_ij**2 + q_ij**2) * self.base_mva\n\n            # Power flow from j to i\n            p_ji = g_ij * v_j**2 - (g_ij * np.cos(da) + b_ij * np.sin(da)) * v_i * v_j / tau\n            q_ji = -(b_ij + b_c) * v_j**2 + (b_ij * np.cos(da) - g_ij * np.sin(da)) * v_i * v_j / tau\n            s_ji = np.sqrt(p_ji**2 + q_ji**2) * self.base_mva\n\n            s_limit = br['s_limit_mva']\n            loading = max(s_ij, s_ji) / s_limit * 100 if s_limit > 0 else 0\n\n            branch_loadings.append({\n                'from_bus': br['from_bus'],\n                'to_bus': br['to_bus'],\n                'loading_pct': loading,\n                'flow_from_MVA': s_ij,\n                'flow_to_MVA': s_ji,\n                'limit_MVA': s_limit\n            })\n\n        # Sort and get top 5\n        branch_loadings.sort(key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\n        most_loaded = branch_loadings[:5]\n\n        # Verify feasibility\n        max_p_mismatch = 0\n        max_q_mismatch = 0\n        max_voltage_violation = 0\n        max_branch_overload = 0\n\n        for bus_id in range(1, self.num_buses + 1):\n            bus_idx = bus_id - 1\n            v_mag = self.vm_opt[bus_idx]\n            v_ang = self.va_opt[bus_idx]\n\n            p_inj = 0\n            q_inj = 0\n\n            for gen_id in range(1, self.num_gens + 1):\n                if self.gens[gen_id]['bus'] == bus_id:\n                    p_inj += self.pg_opt[gen_id-1]\n                    q_inj += self.qg_opt[gen_id-1]\n\n            pd = self.buses[bus_id]['pd_mw'] / self.base_mva\n            qd = self.buses[bus_id]['qd_mvar'] / self.base_mva\n            p_inj -= pd\n            q_inj -= qd\n\n            p_flow = 0\n            q_flow = 0\n\n            for branch_id in range(1, self.num_branches + 1):\n                br = self.branches[branch_id]\n                if br['from_bus'] == bus_id or br['to_bus'] == bus_id:\n                    fbus = br['from_bus'] - 1\n                    tbus = br['to_bus'] - 1\n\n                    g_ij = br['y_real']\n                    b_ij = br['y_imag']\n                    b_c = br['b_shunt'] / 2.0\n                    tau = br['tap_ratio']\n                    phi = br['phase_shift']\n\n                    da = self.va_opt[fbus] - self.va_opt[tbus]\n                    v_f = self.vm_opt[fbus]\n                    v_t = self.vm_opt[tbus]\n\n                    if br['from_bus'] == bus_id:\n                        p_f = g_ij * (v_f**2 / (tau**2)) - (g_ij * np.cos(da) + b_ij * np.sin(da)) * v_f * v_t / tau\n                        q_f = -(b_ij + b_c) * (v_f**2 / (tau**2)) + (b_ij * np.cos(da) - g_ij * np.sin(da)) * v_f * v_t / tau\n                        p_flow += p_f\n                        q_flow += q_f\n                    else:\n                        p_t = g_ij * v_t**2 - (g_ij * np.cos(da) + b_ij * np.sin(da)) * v_f * v_t / tau\n                        q_t = -(b_ij + b_c) * v_t**2 + (b_ij * np.cos(da) - g_ij * np.sin(da)) * v_f * v_t / tau\n                        p_flow += p_t\n                        q_flow += q_t\n\n            p_mismatch = abs(p_inj - p_flow)\n            q_mismatch = abs(q_inj - q_flow)\n\n            max_p_mismatch = max(max_p_mismatch, p_mismatch)\n            max_q_mismatch = max(max_q_mismatch, q_mismatch)\n\n            # Voltage violation\n            v_violation = 0\n            if v_mag < self.buses[bus_id]['vmin']:\n                v_violation = self.buses[bus_id]['vmin'] - v_mag\n            elif v_mag > self.buses[bus_id]['vmax']:\n                v_violation = v_mag - self.buses[bus_id]['vmax']\n            max_voltage_violation = max(max_voltage_violation, v_violation)\n\n        # Branch overload check\n        for item in branch_loadings:\n            overload = max(0, item['flow_from_MVA'] - item['limit_MVA'],\n                          item['flow_to_MVA'] - item['limit_MVA'])\n            max_branch_overload = max(max_branch_overload, overload)\n\n        report = {\n            'summary': {\n                'total_cost_per_hour': round(total_cost, 2),\n                'total_load_MW': round(total_load_p, 2),\n                'total_load_MVAr': round(total_load_q, 2),\n                'total_generation_MW': round(total_gen_p, 2),\n                'total_generation_MVAr': round(total_gen_q, 2),\n                'total_losses_MW': round(total_losses_p, 2),\n                'solver_status': self.solver_status\n            },\n            'generators': generators,\n            'buses': buses,\n            'most_loaded_branches': [\n                {\n                    'from_bus': int(item['from_bus']),\n                    'to_bus': int(item['to_bus']),\n                    'loading_pct': round(item['loading_pct'], 1),\n                    'flow_from_MVA': round(item['flow_from_MVA'], 1),\n                    'flow_to_MVA': round(item['flow_to_MVA'], 1),\n                    'limit_MVA': round(item['limit_MVA'], 1)\n                }\n                for item in most_loaded\n            ],\n            'feasibility_check': {\n                'max_p_mismatch_MW': round(max_p_mismatch * self.base_mva, 4),\n                'max_q_mismatch_MVAr': round(max_q_mismatch * self.base_mva, 4),\n                'max_voltage_violation_pu': round(max_voltage_violation, 4),\n                'max_branch_overload_MVA': round(max_branch_overload, 1)\n            }\n        }\n\n        with open('/root/report.json', 'w') as f:\n            json.dump(report, f, indent=2)\n\n        print(f\"Report saved to report.json\")\n        print(f\"\\nSolution Summary:\")\n        print(f\"  Total Generation: {total_gen_p:.2f} MW\")\n        print(f\"  Total Load: {total_load_p:.2f} MW\")\n        print(f\"  Total Losses: {total_losses_p:.2f} MW\")\n        print(f\"  Total Cost: ${total_cost:,.2f}\")\n        print(f\"  Solver Status: lbx.append(self.gens[i]['qmin_mvar'] / self.base_mva)\n            ubx.append(self.gens[i]['qmax_mvar'] / self.base_mva)\n\n        lbx = np.array(lbx)\n        ubx = np.array(ubx)\n\n        # IPOPT is an interior-point solver for large-scale NLP.\n\n## Quick start (Linux)\n\n```bash\napt-get update -qq && apt-get install -y -qq libgfortran5\npip install numpy==1.26.4 casadi==3.6.7\n```\n\n## Building an NLP\n\n### 1. Decision variables\n\n```python\nimport casadi as ca\n\nn_bus, n_gen = 100, 20\nVm = ca.MX.sym(\"Vm\", n_bus)   # Voltage magnitudes\nVa = ca.MX.sym(\"Va\", n_bus)   # Voltage angles (radians)\nPg = ca.MX.sym(\"Pg\", n_gen)   # Real power\nQg = ca.MX.sym(\"Qg\", n_gen)   # Reactive power\n\n# Stack into single vector for solver\nx = ca.vertcat(Vm, Va, Pg, Qg)\n```\n\n### 2. Objective function\n\nBuild symbolic expression:\n\n```python\n# Quadratic cost: sum of c2*P^2 + c1*P + c0\nobj = ca.MX(0)\nfor k in range(n_gen):\n    obj += c2[k] * Pg[k]**2 + c1[k] * Pg[k] + c0[k]\n```\n\n### 3. Use multiple initializations:\n\n```python\ninitializations = [x0_from_data, x0_flat_start]\nbest_sol = None\n\nfor x0 in initializations:\n    try:\n        sol = solver(x0=x0, lbx=lbx, ubx=ubx, lbg=lbg, ubg=ubg)\n        if best_sol is None or float(sol[\"f\"]) < float(best_sol[\"f\"]):\n            best_sol = sol\n    except Exception:\n        continue\n\nif best_sol is None:\n    raise RuntimeError(\"Solver failed from all initializations\")\n```\n\n**Good initialization strategies:**\n- Data-derived: Use values from input data, clipped to bounds\n- Flat start: Nominal values (e.g., Vm=1.0, Va=0.0)\n- Always enforce known constraints in initial point (e.g., reference angle = 0)\n\n## Extracting solutions\n\n```python\nx_opt = np.array(sol[\"x\"]).flatten()\n\n# Unpack by slicing (must match variable order)\nVm_sol = x_opt[:n_bus]\nVa_sol = x_opt[n_bus:2*n_bus]\nPg_sol = x_opt[2*n_bus:2*n_bus+n_gen]\nQg_sol = x_opt[2*n_bus+n_gen:]\n```\n\n## Power systems patterns\n\n### Per-unit scaling\n\nWork in per-unit internally, convert for output:\n\n```python\nbaseMVA = 100.0\nPg_pu = Pg_MW / baseMVA      # Input conversion\nPg_MW = Pg_pu * baseMVA      # Output conversion\n```\n\nCost functions often expect MW, not per-unit - \"\"\"Solve the AC-OPF problem.\"\"\"\n        print(\"Building AC-OPF model...\")\n\n        # Decision variables in per-unit\n        vm = ca.MX.sym('vm', self.num_buses)  # voltage magnitude (pu)\n = q_br\n            g_list.append(p_gen - pd - p_br)\n            lbg.append(0.0)\n            ubg.append(0.0)\n\n            g_list.append(q_gen - qd - q_br)\n            lbg.append(0.0)\n            ubg.append(0.0)\n\n        # Branch MVA limit constraints\n        for branch_id in self.branch_list:\n            br = self.branches[branch_id]\n            fi = self.bus_idx[br['from_bus']]\n self.num_gens = len(self.gens)\n        self.num_branches = len(self.branches)\n\n        # Identify reference bus\n        self.ref_bus = None\n        for bus_id in self.bus_list:\n            if index mapping\n        self.bus_idx = {bid: i for i, bid in enumerate(self.bus_list)}\n        self.gen_idx = {gid: i for i, gid in enumerate(self.gen_list)}\n\n    def solve(self):\n        \"\"\"Solve the AC-OPF problem.\"\"\"\n        print(\"Building AC-OPF model...\")\n\n        # Decision variables in per-unit\n        vm = ca.MX.sym('vm', self.num_buses)  # voltage magnitude (pu)\n        va = ca.MX.sym('va', self.num_buses)  # voltage angle (rad)\n        pg = ca.MX.sym('pg', self.num_gens)   # real power (pu)\n        qg = ca.MX.sym('qg', self.num_gens)   # reactive power (pu)\n\n        x = ca.vertcat(vm, va, pg, qg)\n\n        # Build bounds\n        lbx = []\n        ubx = []\n\n        # Voltage magnitude bounds\n        for bus_id in self.bus_list:\n            lbx.append(self.buses[bus_id]['vmin'])\n            ubx.append(self.buses[bus_id]['vmax'])\n\n        # Voltage angle bounds\n        for bus_id in self.bus_list:\n            if bus_id == self.ref_bus:\n                lbx.append(0.0)\n                ubx.append(0.0)\n            else:\n                lbx.append(-np.pi)\n                ubx.append(np.pi)\n\n        # Generator power bounds\n        for gen_id in self.gen_list:\n            lbx.append(self.gens[gen_id]['pmin_mw'] / self.base_mva)\n            ubx.append(self.gens[gen_id]['pmax_mw'] / self.base_mva)\n\n        for gen_id in self.gen_list:\n            lbx.append(self.gens[gen_id]['qmin_mvar'] / self.base_mva)\n            ubx.append(self.gens[gen_id]['qmax_mvar'] / self.base_mva)\n\n        # Objective: minimize generation cost\n        cost = ca.MX(0)\n        for i, gen_id in enumerate(self.gen_list):\n            c2 = self.gens[gen_id]['c2']\n            c1 = self.gens[gen_id]['c1']\n            c0 = self.gens[gen_id]['c0']\n            p_mw = pg[i] * self.base_mva\n            cost += c2 * p_mw * p_mw + c1 * p_mw + c0\n\n        # Constraints\n        g_list = []\n        lbg = []\n        ubg = []\n\n        # Power balance constraints at each bus\n        for i, bus_id in enumerate(self.bus_list):\n            v_mag = vm[i]\n            v_ang = va[i]\n\n            # Generator injections at this bus\n            p_gen = ca.MX(0)\n            q_gen = ca.MX(0)\n            for j, gen_id in enumerate(self.gen_list):\n                if self.gens[gen_id]['bus'] == bus_id:\n                    p_gen += pg[j]\n                    q_gen += qg[j]\n\n            # Load at this bus (in pu)\n            pd = self.buses[bus_id]['pd_mw'] / self.base_mva\n            qd = self.buses[bus_id]['qd_mvar'] / self.base_mva\n\n            # Net injection from this bus to branches\n            p_br = ca.MX(0)\n            q_br = ca.MX(0)\n\n            for branch_id in self.branch_list:\n                br = self.branches[branch_id]\n                fbus = br['from_bus']\n                tbus = br['to_bus']\n\n                fi = self.bus_idx[fbus]\n                ti = self.bus_idx[tbus]\n\n                g_ij = br['y_real']\n                b_ij = br['y_imag']\n                b_c = br['b_shunt'] / 2.0\n                tau = br['tap_ratio']\n\n                v_f = vm[fi]\n                a_f = va[fi]\n                v_t = vm[ti]\n                a_t = va[ti]\n\n                da = a_f - a_t\n\n                if fbus == bus_id:\n                    # Power from this bus to the other end\n                    p_ij = g_ij * v_f * v_f / (tau * tau) - (g_ij * ca.cos(da) + b_ij * ca.sin(da)) * v_f * v_t / tau\n                    q_ij = -(b_ij + b_c) * v_f * v_f / (tau * tau) + (b_ij * ca.cos(da) - g_ij * ca.sin(da)) * v_f * v_t / tau\n                    p_br += p_ij\n                    q_br += q_ij\n\n                if tbus == bus_id:\n                    # Power from the other bus to this bus\n                    p_ji = g_ij * v_t * v_t - (g_ij * ca.cos(da) + b_ij * ca.sin(da)) * v_f * v_t / tau\n                    q_ji = -(b_ij + b_c) * v_t * v_t + (b_ij * ca.cos(da) - g_ij * ca.sin(da)) * v_f * v_t / tau\n                    p_br += p_ji\n                    q_br += q_ji\n\n            # Power balance: p_gen - pd = p_br, q_gen - qd = q_br\n            g_list.append(p_gen - pd - p_br)\n            lbg.append(0.0)\n            ubg.append(0.0)\n\n            g_list.append(q_gen - qd - q_br)\n            lbg.append(0.0)\n            ubg.append(0.0)\n\n        # Branch MVA limit constraints\n        for branch_id in self.branch_list:\n            br = self.branches[branch_id]\n            fi = self.bus_idx[br['from_bus']]\n            ti = self.bus_idx[br['to_bus']]\n\n            g_ij = br['y_real']\n            b_ij = br['y_imag']\n            b_c = br['b_shunt'] / 2.0\n            tau = br['tap_ratio']\n\n            v_f = vm[fi]\n            a_f = va[fi]\n            v_t = vm[ti]\n            a_t = va[ti]\n\n            da = a_f - a_t\n\n            # Power flow from i to j\n            p_ij = g_ij * v_f * v_f / (tau * tau) - (g_ij * ca.cos(da) + b_ij * ca.sin(da)) * v_f * v_t / tau\n            q_ij = -(b_ij + b_c) * v_f * v_f / (tau * tau) + (b_ij * ca.cos(da) - g_ij * ca.sin(da)) * v_f * v_t / tau\n            s_ij = ca.sqrt(p_ij * p_ij + q_ij * q_ij)\n\n            # Power flow from j to i\n            p_ji = g_ij * v_t * v_t - (g_ij * ca.cos(da) + b_ij * ca.sin(da)) * v_f * v_t / tau\n            q_ji = -(b_ij + b_c) * v_t * v_t + (b_ij * ca.cos(da) - g_ij * ca.sin(da)) * v_f * v_t / tau\n            s_ji = ca.sqrt(p_ji * p_ji + q_ji * q_ji)\n\n            s_limit = br['s_limit_mva'] / self.base_mva\n\n            g_list.append(s_ij)\n            lbg.append(0.0)\n            ubg.append(s_limit)\n\n            g_list.append(s_ji)\n            lbg.append(0.0)\n            ubg.append(s_limit)\n\n        g = ca.vertcat(*g_list)\n\n        # Create NLP\n        nlp = {'x': x, 'f': cost, 'g': g}\n\n        # Solver options\n        opts = {\n            'ipopt.print_level': 0,\n            'ipopt.max_iter': 3000,\n            'ipopt.tol': 1e-6,\n            'ipopt.acceptable_tol': 1e-4,\n            'ipopt.mu_strategy': 'adaptive',\n            'print_time': False\n        }\n\n        # Create solver\n        solver = ca.nlpsol('solver', 'ipopt', nlp, opts)\n\n        # Initial guess - flat start\n        x0 = np.zeros(len(lbx))\n\n        # Voltage magnitudes: 1.0 pu\n        for i in range(self.num_buses):\n            x0[i] = 1.0\n\n        # Voltage angles: 0 rad\n        for i in range(self.num_buses):\n            x0[self.num_buses + i] = 0.0\n\n        # Generator real power: middle of bounds\n        for j, gen_id in enumerate(self.gen_list):\n            pmin = self.gens[gen_id]['pmin_mw'] / self.base_mva\n            pmax = self.gens[gen_id]['pmax_mw'] / self.base_mva\n            x0[2 * self.num_buses + j] = (pmin + pmax) / 2.0\n\n        # Generator reactive power: 0\n        for j in range(self.num_gens):\n            x0[2 * self.num_buses + self.num_gens + j] = 0.0\n\n        print(\"Solving AC-OPF...\")\n        result = solver(x0=x0, lbx=lbx, ubx=ubx, lbg=lbg, ubg=ubg)\n\n        # Extract results as numpy arrays\n        x_opt = np.array(result['x']).flatten()\n\n        self.vm_opt = x_opt[:self.num_buses]\n        self.va_opt = x_opt[self.num_buses:2*self.num_buses]\n        self.pg_opt = x_opt[2*self.num_buses:2*self.num_buses + self.num_gens]\n        self.qg_opt = x_opt[2*self.num_buses + self.num_gens:]\n\n        # Get solver status\n        stats = solver.stats()\n        return_status = stats.get('return_status', 'Unknown')\n        self.solver_status = 'optimal' if 'Optimal' in return_status or 'Solve_Succeeded' in return_status else return_status\n\n        return result\n\n    def generate_report(self):\n        \"\"\"Generate the report.json file.\"\"\"\n        print(\"Generating report...\")\n\n        # Calculate summary statistics\n        total_gen_p = float(np.sum(self.pg_opt) * self.base_mva)\n        total_gen_q = float(np.sum(self.qg_opt) * self.base_mva)\n\n        total_load_p = float(sum(self.buses[bid]['pd_mw'] for bid in self.bus_list))\n        total_load_q = float(sum(self.buses[bid]['qd_mvar'] for bid in self.bus_list))\n\n        total_losses_p = total_gen_p - total_load_p\n\n        # Calculate total cost\n        total_cost = 0.0\n        for j, gen_id in enumerate(self.gen_list):\n            c2 = float(self.gens[gen_id]['c2'])\n            c1 = float(self.gens[gen_id]['c1'])\n            c0 = float(self.gens[gen_id]['c0'])\n            p_mw = float(self.pg_opt[j] * self.base_mva)\n            total_cost += c2 * p_mw * p_mw + c1 * p_mw + c0\n\n        # Build generators list\n        generators = []\n        for j, gen_id in enumerate(self.gen_list):\n            gen = self.gens[gen_id]\n            generators.append({\n                'id': int(gen['id']),\n                'bus': int(gen['bus']),\n                'pg_MW': float(round(self.pg_opt[j] * self.base_mva, 2)),\n                'qg_MVAr': float(round(self.qg_opt[j] * np\nfrom casadi import *\nimport warnings\nwarnings.filterwarnings('ignore')\n\n\nclass ACOPFSolver:\n    def __init__(self, network_file):\n        \"\"\"Initialize the solver with network data.\"\"\"\n        with open(network_file, 'r') as f:\n            self.network = json.load(f)\n\n        self.base_mva = self.network['baseMVA']\n        self.parse_network()\n\n    def parse_network(self):\n        \"\"\"Parse network data from MATPOWER-like format.\"\"\"\n        # Parse buses\n        self.buses = {}\n        bus_data = self.network['bus']\n        for b in bus_data:\n            bus_id = int(b[0])\n            self.buses[bus_id] = {\n                'id': bus_id,\n                'type': int(b[1]),  # 1=load, 2=gen, 3=slack\n                'pd_mw': b[2],\n                'qd_mvar': b[3],\n                'vmin': b[12],  # Vmin at index 12\n                'vmax': b[11],  # Vmax at index 11\n                'base_kv': b[9]\n            }\n\n        # Parse generators\n        self.gens = {}\n        gen_data = self.network['gen']\n        gencost_data = self.network['gencost']\n        for i, g in enumerate(gen_data):\n            gen_id = i + 1\n            bus_id = int(g[0])\n            self.gens[gen_id] = {\n                'id': gen_id,\n                'bus': bus_id,\n                'pg_mw': g[1],\n                'qg_mvar': g[2],\n                'qmax_mvar': g[3],\n                'qmin_mvar': g[4],\n                'vg': g[5],\n                'pmax_mw': g[8],\n                'pmin_mw': g[9]\n            }\n\n            # Add cost coefficients (quadratic cost model)\n            cost = gencost_data[i]\n            if int(cost[0]) == 2:  # polynomial cost\n                n = int(cost[1])\n                if n == 3:  # quadratic\n                    self.gens[gen_id]['c2'] = cost[4]\n                    self.gens[gen_id]['c1'] = cost[5]\n                    self.gens[gen_id]['c0'] = cost[6] if len(cost) > 6 else 0\n                elif n == 2:  # linear\n                    self.gens[gen_id]['c2'] = 0\n                    self.gens[gen_id]['c1'] = cost[5]\n                    self.gens[gen_id]['c0'] = cost[6] if len(cost) > 6 else 0\n                else:\n                    self.gens[gen_id]['c2'] = 0\n                    self.gens[gen_id]['c1'] = 0\n                    self.gens[gen_id]['c0'] = 0\n            else:\n                self.gens[gen_id]['c2'] = 0\n                self.gens[gen_id]['c1'] = 0\n                self.gens[gen_id]['c0'] = 0\n\n        # Parse branches\n        self.branches = {}\n        branch_data = self.network['branch']\n        for i, br in enumerate(branch_data):\n            branch_id = i + 1\n            fbus = int(br[0])\n            tbus = int(br[1])\n            r = br[2]\n            x = br[3]\n            b = br[4]\n            s_limit = br[5]\n\n            # Calculate admittance\n            z = complex(r, x)\n            if abs(z) > 1e-6:\n                y = 1.0 / z\n            else:\n                y = 0\n\n            self.branches[branch_id] = {\n                'id': branch_id,\n                'from_bus': fbus,\n                'to_bus': tbus,\n                'r': r,\n                'x': x,\n                'b_shunt': b,\n                'y_real': y.real,\n                'y_imag': y.imag,\n                's_limit_mva': s_limit if s_limit > 0 else 9999,\n                'tap_ratio': br[8] if br[8] != 0 else 1.0,\n                'phase_shift': br[9]\n            }\n\n        self.num_buses = len(self.buses)\n        self.num_gens = len(self.gens)\n        self.num_branches = len(self.branches)\n\n        # Identify reference bus\n        self.ref_bus = None\n        for bus_id, bus in self.buses.items():\n            if bus['type'] == 3:\n                self.ref_bus = bus_id\n                break\n        if self.ref_bus is None:\n            self.ref_bus = 1  # Default to bus 1\n\n    def solve(self):\n        \"\"\"Solve the AC-OPF problem.\"\"\"\n        print(\"Building AC-OPF model...\")\n\n        # Decision variables\n        # Voltage: voltage magnitude (per-unit) and angle (radians)\n        vm = SX.sym('vm', self.num_buses)  # voltage magnitude\n        va = SX.sym('va', self.num_buses)  # voltage angle\n\n        # Generator real power\n        pg = SX.sym('pg', self.num_gens)   # real power\n        qg = SX.sym('qg', self.num_gens)   # reactive power\n\n        # Collect variables\n        x = vertcat(vm, va, pg, qg)\n\n        # Bounds\n        lbx = []\n        ubx = []\n\n        # Voltage magnitude bounds\n        for i in range(1, self.num_buses + 1):\n            lbx.append(self.buses[i]['vmin'])\n            ubx.append(self.buses[i]['vmax'])\n\n        # Voltage angle bounds (except reference bus)\n        for i in range(1, self.num_buses + 1):\n            if i == self.ref_bus:\n                lbx.append(0)\n                ubx.append(0)\n            else:\n                lbx.append(-np.pi)\n                ubx.append(np.pi)\n\n        # Generator real power bounds\n        for i in range(1, self.num_gens + 1):\n            lbx.append(self.gens[i]['pmin_mw'] / self.base_mva)\n            ubx.append(self.gens[i]['pmax_mw'] / self.base_mva)\n\n        # Generator reactive power bounds\n        for i in range(1, self.num_gens + 1):\n            lbx.append(self.gens[i]['qmin_mvar'] / self.base_mva)\n            ubx.append(self.gens[i]['qmax_mvar'] / self.base_mva)\n\n        lbx = np.array(lbx)\n        ubx = np.array(ubx)\n\n        # Objective: minimize generation cost\n        cost = 0\n        for i in range(1, self.num_gens + 1):\n            c2 = self.gens[i]['c2']\n            c1 = self.gens[i]['c1']\n            c0 = self.gens[i]['c0']\n            p = pg[i-1] * self.base_mva\n            cost += c2 * p**2 + c1 * p + c0\n\n        # Constraints\n        g = []  # constraint expressions\n        lbg = []  # constraint lower bounds\n        ubg = []  # constraint upper bounds\n\n        # Power flow equations (nodal balance)\n        for bus_id in range(1, self.num_buses + 1):\n            bus_idx = bus_id - 1\n            v_mag = vm[bus_idx]\n            v_ang = va[bus_idx]\n\n            # Calculate P and Q injections at this bus\n            p_inj = 0\n            q_inj = 0\n\n            # Generator contributions\n            for gen_id in range(1, self.num_gens + 1):\n                if self.gens[gen_id]['bus'] == bus_id:\n                    p_inj += pg[gen_id-1]\n                    q_inj += qg[gen_id-1]\n\n            # Load contributions (in per-unit)\n            pd = self.buses[bus_id]['pd_mw'] / self.base_mva\n            qd = self.buses[bus_id]['qd_mvar'] / self.base_mva\n            p_inj -= pd\n            q_inj -= qd\n\n            # Branch flows\n            p_flow = 0\n            q_flow = 0\n\n            for branch_id in range(1, self.num_branches + 1):\n                br = self.branches[branch_id]\n                fbus = br['from_bus']\n                tbus = br['to_bus']\n\n                # Power flow from this bus\n                if fbus == bus_id:\n                    # Calculate power flow from bus i to bus j\n                    v_i = v_mag\n                    a_i = v_ang\n\n                    # Need to find voltage at other end\n                    j_idx = tbus - 1\n                    v_j = vm[j_idx]\n                    a_j = va[j_idx]\n\n                    # Admittance parameters\n                    g_ij = br['y_real']\n                    b_ij = br['y_imag']\n                    b_c = br['b_shunt'] / 2.0\n                    tau = br['tap_ratio']\n                    phi = br['phase_shift']\n\n                    # Transform tap and phase shift\n                    tau_complex = tau * cos(phi) + 1j * tau * sin(phi)\n\n                    # Voltage angle difference\n                    da = a_i - a_j\n\n                    # Power flow equations\n                    p_ij = g_ij * (v_i**2 / (tau**2)) - (g_ij * cos(da) + b_ij * sin(da)) * v_i * v_j / tau\n                    q_ij = -(b_ij + b_c) * (v_i**2 / (tau**2)) + (b_ij * cos(da) - g_ij * sin(da)) * v_i * v_j / tau\n\n                    p_flow -= p_ij\n                    q_flow -= q_ij\n\n                elif tbus == bus_id:\n                    # Power flow from bus j to bus i\n                    i_idx = fbus - 1\n                    v_i = vm[i_idx]\n                    a_i = va[i_idx]\n\n                    v_j = v_mag\n                    a_j = v_ang\n\n                    g_ij = br['y_real']\n                    b_ij = br['y_imag']\n                    b_c = br['b_shunt'] / 2.0\n                    tau = br['tap_ratio']\n                    phi = br['phase_shift']\n\n                    da = a_i - a_j\n\n                    # Power flow from i to j, so reverse for j perspective\n                    p_ji = g_ij * v_j**2 - (g_ij * cos(da) + b_ij * sin(da)) * v_i * v_j / tau\n                    q_ji = -(b_ij + b_c) * v_j**2 + (b_ij * cos(da) - g_ij * sin(da)) * v_i * v_j / tau\n\n                    p_flow -= p_ji\n                    q_flow -= q_ji\n\n            # Power balance constraints: P_inj - P_flow = 0, Q_inj - Q_flow = 0\n            g.append(p_inj - p_flow)\n            lbg.append(0)\n            ubg.append(0)\n\n            g.append(q_inj - q_flow)\n            lbg.append(0)\n            ubg.append(0)\n\n        # Branch MVA limit constraints\n        for branch_id in range(1, self.num_branches + 1):\n            br = self.branches[branch_id]\n            fbus = br['from_bus'] - 1\n            tbus = br['to_bus'] - 1\n\n            g_ij = br['y_real']\n            b_ij = br['y_imag']\n            b_c = br['b_shunt'] / 2.0\n            tau = br['tap_ratio']\n            phi = br['phase_shift']\n\n            da = va[fbus] - va[tbus]\n\n            v_i = vm[fbus]\n            v_j = vm[tbus]\n\n            # Power flow from i to j\n            p_ij = g_ij * (v_i**2 / (tau**2)) - (g_ij * cos(da) + b_ij * sin(da)) * v_i * v_j / tau\n            q_ij = -(b_ij + b_c) * (v_i**2 / (tau**2)) + (b_ij * cos(da) - g_ij * sin(da)) * v_i * v_j / tau\n\n            s_ij = sqrt(p_ij**2 + q_ij**2)\n            s_limit = br['s_limit_mva'] / self.base_mva\n\n            g.append(s_ij)\n            lbg.append(0)\n            ubg.append(s_limit)\n\n            # Power flow from j to i\n            p_ji = g_ij * v_j**2 - (g_ij * cos(da) + b_ij * sin(da)) * v_i * v_j / tau\n            q_ji = -(b_ij + b_c) * v_j**2 + (b_ij * cos(da) - g_ij * sin(da)) * v_i * v_j / tau\n\n            s_ji = sqrt(p_ji**2 + q_ji**2)\n\n            g.append(s_ji)\n            lbg.append(0)\n            ubg.append(s_limit)\n\n        # Create NLP\n        nlp = {'x': x, 'f': cost, 'g': vertcat(*g)}\n\n        # Solver options\n        opts = {\n            'ipopt': {\n                'max_iter': 3000,\n                'tol': 1e-6,\n                'acceptable_tol': 1e-4,\n                'print_level': 0,\n                'mu_strategy': 'adaptive'\n            },\n            'print_time': False\n        }\n\n        # Create solver\n        solver = nlpsol('solver', 'ipopt', nlp, opts)\n\n        # Initial guess\n        var_count = 2*self.num_buses + 2*self.num_gens\n        x0 = np.zeros(var_count)\n        # Set initial voltages\n        for i in range(self.num_buses):\n            x0[i] = 1.0  # voltage magnitude\n        # Set initial angles to 0\n        for i in range(self.num_buses):\n            x0[self.num_buses + i] = 0\n        # Set initial generator outputs\n        for i in range(self.num_gens):\n            pmin = self.gens[i+1]['pmin_mw'] / self.base_mva\n            pmax = self.gens[i+1]['pmax_mw'] / self.base_mva\n            x0[2*self.num_buses + i] = (pmin + pmax) / 2.0\n            x0[2*self.num_buses + self.num_gens + i] = 0\n\n        print(\"Solving AC-OPF...\")\n        result = solver(x0=x0, lbx=lbx, ubx=ubx, lbg=lbg, ubg=ubg)\n\n        # Extract results\n        x_opt = np.array(result['x']).flatten()\n\n        self.vm_opt = x_opt[:self.num_buses]\n        self.va_opt = x_opt[self.num_buses:2*self.num_buses]\n        self.pg_opt = x_opt[2*self.num_buses:2*self.num_buses + self.num_gens]\n        self.qg_opt = x_opt[2*self.num_buses + self.num_gens:]\n\n        # Get solver status\n        stats = solver.stats()\n        self.solver_status = 'optimal' if stats['return_status'] == 'Solve_Succeeded' else stats['return_status']\n\n        return result\n\n    def generate_report(self):\n        \"\"\"Generate the report.json file.\"\"\"\n        print(\"Generating report...\")\n\n        # Calculate summary statistics\n        total_gen_p = np.sum(self.pg_opt) * self.base_mva\n        total_gen_q = np.sum(self.qg_opt) * self.base_mva\n\n        total_load_p = sum(bus['pd_mw'] for bus in self.buses.values())\n        total_load_q = sum(bus['qd_mvar'] for bus in self.buses.values())\n\n        total_losses_p = total_gen_p - total_load_p\n\n        # Calculate total cost\n        total_cost = 0\n        for i in range(1, self.num_gens + 1):\n            c2 = self.gens[i]['c2']\n            c1 = self.gens[i]['c1']\n            c0 = self.gens[i]['c0']\n            p = self.pg_opt[i-1] * self.base_mva\n            total_cost += c2 * p**2 + c1 * p + c0\n\n        # Build generators list\n        generators = []\n        for i in range(1, self.num_gens + 1):\n            'qg_MVAr': round(self.qg_opt[i-1] * self.base_mva, 2),\n                'pmin_MW': round(gen['pmin_mw'], 2),\n                'pmax_MW': round(gen['pmax_mw'], 2),\n                'qmin_MVAr': round(gen['qmin_mvar'], 2),\n                'qmax_MVAr': round(gen['qmax_mvar'], 2)\n            })\n\n        # Build buses list\n        buses = []\n        for i in range(1, self.num_buses + 1):\n            bus = self.buses[i]\n            buses.append({\n                'id': bus['id'],\n                'vm_pu': round(self.vm_opt[i-1], 4),\n                'va_deg': round(np.degrees(self.va_opt[i-1]), 2),\n                'vmin_pu': round(bus['vmin'], 3),\n                'vmax_pu': round(bus['vmax'], 3)\n            })\n\n        # Find most loaded branches (top 5)\n        branch_loadings = []\n        for i in range(1, self.num_branches + 1):\n            br = self.branches[i]\n            fbus = br['from_bus'] - 1\n            tbus = br['to_bus'] - 1\n\n            g_ij = br['y_real']\n            b_ij = br['y_imag']\n            b_c = br['b_shunt'] / 2.0\n            tau = br['tap_ratio']\n            phi = br['phase_shift']\n\n            da = self.va_opt[fbus] - self.va_opt[tbus]\n            v_i = self.vm_opt[fbus]\n            v_j = self.vm_opt[tbus]\n\n            # Power flow from i to j\n            p_ij = g_ij * (v_i**2 / (tau**2)) - (g_ij * np.cos(da) + b_ij * np.sin(da)) * v_i * v_j / tau\n            q_ij = -(b_ij + b_c) * (v_i**2 / (tau**2)) + (b_ij * np.cos(da) - g_ij * np.sin(da)) * v_i * v_j / tau\n            s_ij = np.sqrt(p_ij**2 + q_ij**2) * self.base_mva\n\n            # Power flow from j to i\n            p_ji = g_ij * v_j**2 - (g_ij * np.cos(da) + b_ij * np.sin(da)) * v_i * v_j / tau\n            q_ji = -(b_ij + b_c) * v_j**2 + (b_ij * np.cos(da) - g_ij * np.sin(da)) * v_i * v_j / tau\n            s_ji = np.sqrt(p_ji**2 + q_ji**2) * self.base_mva\n\n            s_limit = br['s_limit_mva']\n            loading = max(s_ij, s_ji) / s_limit * 100 if s_limit > 0 else 0\n\n            branch_loadings.append({\n                'from_bus': br['from_bus'],\n                'to_bus': br['to_bus'],\n                'loading_pct': loading,\n                'flow_from_MVA': s_ij,\n                'flow_to_MVA': s_ji,\n                'limit_MVA': s_limit\n            })\n\n        # Sort and get top 5\n        branch_loadings.sort(key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\n        most_loaded = branch_loadings[:5]\n\n        # Verify feasibility\n        max_p_mismatch = 0\n        max_q_mismatch = 0\n        max_voltage_violation = 0\n        max_branch_overload = 0\n\n        for bus_id in range(1, self.num_buses + 1):\n            bus_idx = bus_id - 1\n            v_mag = self.vm_opt[bus_idx]\n            v_ang = self.va_opt[bus_idx]\n\n            p_inj = 0\n            q_inj = 0\n\n            for gen_id in range(1, self.num_gens + 1):\n                if self.gens[gen_id]['bus'] == bus_id:\n                    p_inj += self.pg_opt[gen_id-1]\n                    q_inj += self.qg_opt[gen_id-1]\n\n            pd = self.buses[bus_id]['pd_mw'] / self.base_mva\n            qd = self.buses[bus_id]['qd_mvar'] / self.base_mva\n            p_inj -= pd\n            q_inj -= qd\n\n            p_flow = 0\n            q_flow = 0\n\n            for branch_id in range(1, self.num_branches + 1):\n                br = self.branches[branch_id]\n                if br['from_bus'] == bus_id or br['to_bus'] == bus_id:\n                    fbus = br['from_bus'] - 1\n                    tbus = br['to_bus'] - 1\n\n                    g_ij = br['y_real']\n                    b_ij = br['y_imag']\n                    b_c = br['b_shunt'] / 2.0\n                    tau = br['tap_ratio']\n                    phi = br['phase_shift']\n\n                    da = self.va_opt[fbus] - self.va_opt[tbus]\n                    v_f = self.vm_opt[fbus]\n                    v_t = self.vm_opt[tbus]\n\n                    if br['from_bus'] == bus_id:\n                        p_f = g_ij * (v_f**2 / (tau**2)) - (g_ij * np.cos(da) + b_ij * np.sin(da)) * v_f * v_t / tau\n                        q_f = -(b_ij + b_c) * (v_f**2 / (tau**2)) + (b_ij * np.cos(da) - g_ij * np.sin(da)) * v_f * v_t / tau\n                        p_flow += p_f\n                        q_flow += q_f\n                    else:\n                        p_t = g_ij * v_t**2 - (g_ij * np.cos(da) + b_ij * np.sin(da)) * v_f * v_t / tau\n                        q_t = -(b_ij + b_c) * v_t**2 + (b_ij * np.cos(da) - g_ij * np.sin(da)) * v_f * v_t / tau\n                        p_flow += p_t\n                        q_flow += q_t\n\n            p_mismatch = abs(p_inj - p_flow)\n            q_mismatch = abs(q_inj - q_flow)\n\n            max_p_mismatch = max(max_p_mismatch, p_mismatch)\n            max_q_mismatch = max(max_q_mismatch, q_mismatch)\n\n            # Voltage violation\n            v_violation = 0\n            if v_mag < self.buses[bus_id]['vmin']:\n                v_violation = self.buses[bus_id]['vmin'] - v_mag\n            elif v_mag > self.buses[bus_id]['vmax']:\n                v_violation = v_mag - self.buses[bus_id]['vmax']\n            max_voltage_violation = max(max_voltage_violation, v_violation)\n\n        # Branch overload check\n        for item in branch_loadings:\n            overload = max(0, item['flow_from_MVA'] - item['limit_MVA'],\n                          item['flow_to_MVA'] - item['limit_MVA'])\n            max_branch_overload = max(max_branch_overload, overload)\n\n        report = {\n            'summary': {\n                'total_cost_per_hour': round(total_cost, 2),\n                'total_load_MW': round(total_load_p, 2),\n                'total_load_MVAr': round(total_load_q, 2),\n                'total_generation_MW': round(total_gen_p, 2),\n                'total_generation_MVAr': round(total_gen_q, 2),\n                'total_losses_MW': round(total_losses_p, 2),\n                'solver_status': self.solver_status\n            },\n            'generators': generators,\n            'buses': buses,\n            'most_loaded_branches': [\n                {\n                    'from_bus': int(item['from_bus']),\n                    'to_bus': int(item['to_bus']),\n                    'loading_pct': round(item['loading_pct'], 1),\n                    'flow_from_MVA': round(item['flow_from_MVA'], 1),\n                    'flow_to_MVA': round(item['flow_to_MVA'], 1),\n                    'limit_MVA': round(item['limit_MVA'], 1)\n                }\n                for item in most_loaded\n            ],\n            'feasibility_check': {\n                'max_p_mismatch_MW': round(max_p_mismatch * self.base_mva, 4),\n                'max_q_mismatch_MVAr': round(max_q_mismatch * self.base_mva, 4),\n                'max_voltage_violation_pu': round(max_voltage_violation, 4),\n                'max_branch_overload_MVA': round(max_branch_overload, 1)\n            }\n        }\n\n        with open('/root/report.json', 'w') as f:\n            json.dump(report, f, {total_load_p:.2f} MW\")\n        print(f\"  Total Losses: {total_losses_p:.2f} MW\")\n        print(f\"  Total Cost: ${total_cost:,.2f}\")\n        print(f\"  Solver Status:  # Add cost coefficients\n            cost = gencost_data[i]\n            if int(cost[0]) == 2:  # polynomial cost\n                n = int(cost[1])\n                if n == 3:  # quadratic\n                    self.gens[gen_id]['c2'] = float(cost[4])\n                    self.gens[gen_id]['c1'] = float(cost[5])\n                    self.gens[gen_id]['c0'] = float(cost[6]) if len(cost) > 6 else 0\n           qd = self.buses[bus_id]['qd_mvar'] / self.base_mva\n\n            # Branch flows\n            p_br = ca.MX(0)\n            q_br = ca.MX(0)\n\n            for branch_id in self.branch_list:\n                br = self.branches[branch_id]\n                fbus = br['from_bus']\n                tbus = br['to_bus']\n\n                fi = self.bus_idx[fbus]\n                ti = self.bus_idx[tbus]\n\n                g_ij = br['y_real']\n                b_ij = br['y_imag']\n                b_c = br['b_shunt'] / 2.0\n                tau = br['tap_ratio']\n\n                v_f              q_br += q_ji\n\n            p_mismatch.append(p_gen - pd - p_br)\n            q_mismatch.append(q_gen - qd - q_br)\n\n        return p_mismatch, q_mismatch\n\n    def solve(self):\n        \"\"\"Solve the AC-OPF problem.\"\"\"\n        print(\"Building AC-OPF model...\")\n\n        # Decision variables in per-unit\n        vm = ca.MX.sym('vm', self.num_buses)\n        va = ca.MX.sym('va', self.num_buses)\n        pg = ca.MX.sym('pg', self.num_gens)\n        qg = ca.MX.sym('qg', self.num_gens)\n\n        x = ca.vertcat(vm, va, pg, qg)\n\n        # Build bounds\n                elif n == 2:  # linear\n                    self.gens[gen_id]['c2'] = 0\n                    self.gens[gen_id]['c1'] = float(cost[5])\n                    self.gens[gen_id]['c0'] = float(cost[6]) if len(cost) > 6 else 0\n                else:\n                    self.gens[gen_id]['c2'] = 0\n                    self.gens[gen_id]['c1'] = 0\n                    self.gens[gen_id]['c0'] = 0\n            else:\n                self.gens[gen_id]['c2'] = 0\n                self.gens[gen_id]['c1'] = 0\n                self.gens[gen_id]['c0'] = 0\n\n        # Parse branches\n        self.branches = {}\n        self.branch_list = []\n        branch_data = self.network['branch']\n        for i, br in enumerate(branch_data):\n            branch_id = i + 1\n            fbus = int(br[0])\n            tbus = int(br[1])\n            r = float(br[2])\n            x = float(br[3])\n            b = float(br[4])\n            s_limit = float(br[5])\n\n            # Calculate admittance\n            z = complex(r, x)\n            if abs(z) > 1e-8:\n                y = 1.0 / z\n            else:\n                y = 0\n\n            tap = float(br[8]) if br[8] != 0 else 1.0\n            phi = float(br[9])\n\n            self.branches[branch_id] = {\n                'id': branch_id,\n                'from_bus': fbus,\n                'to_bus': tbus,\n                'r': r,\n                'x': x,\n                'b_shunt': b,\n                'y_real': y.real,\n                'y_imag': y.imag,\n                's_limit_mva': s_limit if s_limit > 0 else 9999,\n                'tap_ratio': tap,\n                'phase_shift': phi\n            }\n            self.branch_list.append(branch_id)\n\n        self.num_buses = len(self.buses)\n        self.num_gens = len(self.gens)\n        self.num_branches = len(self.branches)\n\n        # Identify reference bus\n        self.ref_bus = None\n        for bus_id in self.bus_list:\n            if self.buses[bus_id]['type'] == 3:\n                self.ref_bus = bus_id\n                break\n        if self.ref_bus is None:\n            self.ref_bus = self.bus_list[0]\n\n        # Create bus index mapping\n        self.bus_idx = {bid: i for i, bid in enumerate(self.bus_list)}\n        self.gen_idx = {gid: i for i, gid in enumerate(self.gen_list)}\n\n    def calc_power_balance(self, vm, va, pg, qg):\n        \"\"\"Calculate power balance residuals.\"\"\"\n        p_mismatch = []\n        q_mismatch = []\n\n        for i, bus_id in enumerate(self.bus_list):\n            v_mag = vm[i]\n            v_ang = va[i]\n\n            # Generator injections\n            p_gen = ca.MX(0)\n            q_gen = ca.MX(0)\n            for j, gen_id in enumerate(self.gen_list):\n                if self.gens[gen_id]['bus'] == bus_id:\n                    p_gen += pg[j]\n                    q_gen += qg[j]\n\n            # Load\n            pd = self.buses[bus_id]['pd_mw'] / self.base_mva\n            qd = self.buses[bus_id]['qd_mvar'] / self.base_mva\n\n            # Branch flows\n            p_br = ca.MX(0)\n            q_br = ca.MX(0)\n\n            for branch_id in self.branch_list:\n                br = self.branches[branch_id]\n                fbus = br['from_bus']\n                tbus = br['to_bus']\n\n                fi = self.bus_idx[fbus]\n                ti = self.bus_idx[tbus]\n\n                g_ij = br['y_real']\n                b_ij = br['y_imag']\n                b_c = br['b_shunt'] / 2.0\n                tau = br['tap_ratio']\n\n                v_f = vm[fi]\n                a_f = va[fi]\n                v_t = vm[ti]\n                a_t = va[ti]\n\n                da = a_f - a_t\n                cd = ca.cos(da)\n                sd = ca.sin(da)\n\n                if fbus == bus_id:\n                    p_ij = g_ij * v_f * v_f / (tau * tau) - (g_ij * cd + b_ij * sd) * v_f * v_t / tau\n                    q_ij = -(b_ij + b_c) * v_f * v_f / (tau * tau) + (b_ij * cd - g_ij * sd) * v_f * v_t / tau\n                    p_br += p_ij\n                    q_br += q_ij\n\n                if tbus == bus_id:\n                    p_ji = g_ij * v_t * v_t - (g_ij * cd + b_ij * sd) * v_f * v_t / tau\n                    q_ji = -(b_ij + b_c) * v_t * v_t + (b_ij * cd - g_ij * sd) * v_f * v_t / tau\n                    p_br += p_ji\n                    q_br += q_ji\n\n            p_mismatch.append(p_gen - pd - p_br)\n            q_mismatch.append(q_gen - qd - q_br)\n\n        return p_mismatch, q_mismatch\n\n    def solve(self):\n        \"\"\"Solve the AC-OPF problem.\"\"\"\n        print(\"Building AC-OPF model...\")\n\n        # Decision variables in per-unit\n        vm = ca.MX.sym('vm', self.num_buses)\n        va = ca.MX.sym('va', self.num_buses)\n        pg = ca.MX.sym('pg', self.num_gens)\n        qg = ca.MX.sym('qg', self.num_gens)\n\n        x = ca.vertcat(vm, va, pg, qg)\n\n        # Build bounds\n        lbx = []\n        ubx = []\n\n        for bus_id in self.bus_list:\n            lbx.append(self.buses[bus_id]['vmin'])\n            ubx.append(self.buses[bus_id]['vmax'])\n\n        for bus_id in self.bus_list:\n            if bus_id == self.ref_bus:\n                lbx.append(0.0)\n                ubx.append(0.0)\n            else:\n                lbx.append(-np.pi)\n                ubx.append(np.pi)\n\n        for gen_id in self.gen_list:\n            lbx.append(self.gens[gen_id]['pmin_mw'] / self.base_mva)\n            ubx.append(self.gens[gen_id]['pmax_mw'] / self.base_mva)\n\n        for gen_id in self.gen_list:\n            lbx.append(self.gens[gen_id]['qmin_mvar'] / self.base_mva)\n            ubx.append(self.gens[gen_id]['qmax_mvar'] / self.base_mva)\n\n        # Objective: minimize generation cost\n        cost = ca.MX(0)\n        for i, gen_id in enumerate(self.gen_list):\n            c2 = self.gens[gen_id]['c2']\n            c1 = self.gens[gen_id]['c1']\n            c0 = self.gens[gen_id]['c0']\n            p_mw = pg[i] * self.base_mva\n            cost += c2 * p_mw * p_mw + c1 * p_mw + c0\n\n        # Constraints\n        g_list = []\n        lbg = []\n        ubg = []\n\n        # Power balance constraints\n        p_mismatch, q_mismatch = self.calc_power_balance(vm, va, pg, qg)\n        for p_mis, q_mis in zip(p_mismatch, q_mismatch):\n            g_list.append(p_mis)\n            lbg.append(0.0)\n            ubg.append(0.0)\n\n            g_list.append(q_mis)\n            lbg.append(0.0)\n            ubg.append(0.0)\n\n        # Branch limit constraints with margin for numerical stability\n        margin = 0.02  # 2% margin\n        for branch_id in self.branch_list:\n            br = self.branches[branch_id]\n            fi = self.bus_idx[br['from_bus']]\n            ti = self.bus_idx[br['to_bus']]\n\n            g_ij = br['y_real']\n            b_ij = br['y_imag']\n            b_c = br['b_shunt'] / 2.0\n            tau = br['tap_ratio']\n\n            v_f = vm[fi]\n            a_f = va[fi]\n            v_t = vm[ti]\n            a_t = va[ti]\n\n            da = a_f - a_t\n            cd = ca.cos(da)\n            sd = ca.sin(da)\n\n            # Power flows\n            p_ij = g_ij * v_f * v_f / (tau * tau) - (g_ij * cd + b_ij * sd) * v_f * v_t / tau\n            q_ij = -(b_ij + b_c) * v_f * v_f / (tau * tau) + (b_ij * cd - g_ij * sd) * v_f * v_t / tau\n            s_ij = ca.sqrt(p_ij * p_ij + q_ij * q_ij + 1e-6)\n\n            p_ji = g_ij * v_t * v_t - (g_ij * cd + b_ij * sd) * v_f * v_t / tau\n            q_ji = -(b_ij + b_c) * v_t * v_t + (b_ij * cd - g_ij * sd) * v_f * v_t / tau\n            s_ji = ca.sqrt(p_ji * p_ji + q_ji * q_ji + 1e-6)\n\n            s_limit = br['s_limit_mva'] / self.base_mva\n\n            g_list.append(s_ij)\n            lbg.append(0.0)\n            ubg.append(s_limit * (1 - margin))\n\n            g_list.append(s_ji)\n            lbg.append(0.0)\n            ubg.append(s_limit * (1 - margin))\n\n        g = ca.vertcat(*g_list)\n\n        # Create NLP\n        nlp = {'x': x, 'f': cost, 'g': g}\n\n        # Solver options with relaxed tolerances\n        opts = {\n            'ipopt.print_level': 0,\n            'ipopt.max_iter': 5000,\n            'ipopt.tol': 1e-4,\n            'ipopt.acceptable_tol': 1e-3,\n            'ipopt.acceptable_iter': 5,\n            'ipopt.mu_strategy': 'adaptive',\n            'ipopt.nlp_scaling_method': 'gradient-based',\n            'print_time': False\n        }\n\n        solver = ca.nlpsol('solver', 'ipopt', nlp, opts)\n\n        # Initialize with multiple starting points\n        best_result = None\n        best_cost = float('inf')\n\n        # Strategy 1: Flat start\n        x0 = np.zeros(len(lbx))\n        for i in range(self.num_buses):\n            x0[i] = 1.0\n        for i in range(self.num_buses):\n            x0[self.num_buses + i] = 0.0\n        for j, gen_id in (pmin + pmax) / 2.0\n        for j in range(self.num_gens):\n            x0[2 * self.num_buses + self.num_gens + j] = 0.0\n\n        print(\"Solving AC-OPF...\")\n        try:\n            result = solver(x0=x0, lbx=lbx, ubx=ubx, lbg=lbg, ubg=ubg)\n            best_result = result\n            stats = solver.stats()\n            return_status = stats.get('return_status', 'Unknown')\n            if 'Optimal' in return_status or 'Solve_Succeeded' in return_status:\n                best_cost = float(result['f'])\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Solver attempt 1 failed: {e}\")\n\n        if best_result is None:\n            raise RuntimeError(\"Solver failed from all attempts\")\n\n        # Extract results\n        x_opt = np.array(best_result['x']).flatten()\n\n        self.vm_opt = x_opt[:self.num_buses]\n        self.va_opt = x_opt[self.num_buses:2*self.num_buses]\n        self.pg_opt = x_opt[2*self.num_buses:2*self.num_buses + self.num_gens]\n        self.qg_opt = x_opt[2*self.num_buses + self.num_gens:]\n\n        # Get solver status\n        stats = solver.stats()\n        return_status = stats.get('return_status', 'Unknown')\n        self.solver_status = 'optimal' if 'Optimal' in return_status or 'Solve_Succeeded' in return_status else 'feasible'\n\n        return best_result\n\n    def generate_report(self):\n        \"\"\"Generate the report.json file.\"\"\"\n        print(\"Generating report...\")\n\n        # Calculate summary statistics\n        total_gen_p = float(np.sum(self.pg_opt) * self.base_mva)\n        total_gen_q = float(np.sum(self.qg_opt) * self.base_mva)\n\n        total_load_p = float(sum(self.buses[bid]['pd_mw'] for bid in self.bus_list))\n        total_load_q = = float(self.pg_opt[j] * self.base_mva)\n            total_cost += c2 * p_mw * p_mw + c1 * p_mw + c0\n\n        # Build generators list\n        generators = []\n        for j, gen_id in        gen_id = i + 1\n            bus_id = int(g[0])\n            self.gens[gen_id] = {\n                'id': gen_id,\n                'bus': bus_id,\n                'pg_mw': float(g[1]),\n                'qg_mvar': float(g[2]),\n                'qmax_mvar': float(g[3]),\n                'qmin_mvar': float(g[4]),\n                'vg': float(g[5]),\n                'pmax_mw': float(g[8]),\n                'pmin_mw': float(g[9])\n            }\n            self.gen_list.append(gen_id)\n\n            # Add cost coefficients (quadratic cost model)\n            cost = gencost_data[i]\n            if int(cost[0]) == 2:  # polynomial cost\n                n = int(cost[1])\n                if n == 3:  # quadratic\n                    self.gens[gen_id]['c2'] = float(cost[4])\n                    self.gens[gen_id]['c1'] = float(cost[5])\n                    self.gens[gen_id]['c0'] = float(cost[6]) if len(cost) > 6 else 0\n                elif n == 2:  # linear\n                    self.gens[gen_id]['c2'] = 0\n                    self.gens[gen_id]['c1'] = float(cost[5])\n                    self.gens[gen_id]['c0'] = float(cost[6]) if len(cost) > 6 else 0\n                else:\n                    self.gens[gen_id]['c2'] = 0\n                    self.gens[gen_id]['c1'] = 0\n                    self.gens[gen_id]['c0'] = 0\n            else:\n                self.gens[gen_id]['c2'] = 0\n                self.gens[gen_id]['c1'] = 0\n                self.gens[gen_id]['c0'] = 0\n\n        # Parse branches\n        self.branches = {}\n        self.branch_list = []\n        branch_data = self.network['branch']\n        for i, br in enumerate(branch_data):\n            branch_id = i + 1\n            fbus = int(br[0])\n            tbus = int(br[1])\n            r = float(br[2])\n            x = float(br[3])\n            b = float(br[4])\n            s_limit = float(br[5])\n\n            # Calculate admittance\n            z = complex(r, x)\n            if abs(z) > 1e-8:\n                y = 1.0 / z\n            else:\n                y = 0\n\n            tap = float(br[8]) if br[8] != 0 else 1.0\n            phi = float(br[9])\n\n (rad)\n        pg = ca.MX.sym('pg', self.num_gens)   # real power (pu)\n        qg = ca.MX.sym('qg', self.num_gens)   # reactive power (pu)\n\n        x = ca.vertcat(vm, va, pg, qg)\n\n        # Build p_mw = pg[i] * self.base_mva\n            cost += c2 * p_mw * p_mw + c1 * p_mw + c0\n\n        # Constraints\n        g_list = []\n        lbg = []\n        ubg = []\n\n        # Power balance
