g_list.append(p_gen - pd - p_br)\n            lbg.append(0.0)\n            ubg.append(0.0)\n\n            g_list.append(q_gen - qd - q_br)\n            lbg.append(0.0)\n            ubg.append(0.0)\n\n self.bus_idx[br['to_bus']]\n\n            g_ij = br['y_real']\n            b_ij = br['y_imag']\n            b_c = br['b_shunt'] / 2.0\n            tau = br['tap_ratio']\n\n            v_f = vm[fi]\n ca.vertcat(*g_list)\n\n        # Create NLP\n        nlp = {'x': x, 'f': cost, 'g': g}\n\n        # Solver options\n        opts = {\n            'ipopt.print_level': 0,\n            'ipopt.max_iter': 3000,\n            'ipopt.tol': 1e-6,\n            'ipopt.acceptable_tol': 1e-4,\n            'ipopt.mu_strategy': 'adaptive',\n            'print_time': False\n        }\n\n        # Create solver\n x_opt[2*self.num_buses:2*self.num_buses + self.num_gens]\n        self.qg_opt = x_opt[2*self.num_buses + self.num_gens:]\n\n        # Get solver status\n        stats = solver.stats()\n      total_load_q = float(sum(self.buses[bid]['qd_mvar'] for bid in self.bus_list))\n\n        total_losses_p = total_gen_p - total_load_p\n\n        # Calculate total cost\n        total_cost = 0.0\n        for j, gen_id in enumerate(self.gen_list):\n            c2 = float(self.gens[gen_id]['c2'])\n            c1 = float(self.gens[gen_id]['c1'])\n            c0 = float(self.gens[gen_id]['c0'])\n     else:\n                pg[j] = (pmin + pmax) / 2.0\n\n        # Set all reactive power to 0\n        qg = np.zeros(self.num_gens)\n\n        return vm, va, pg, qg\n\n    def calc_mismatch(self, vm, va, pg, qg):\n        \"\"\"Calculate power balance mismatch for a given point.\"\"\"\n        p_mis = []\n        q_mis = []\n\n        for i, bus_id in enumerate(self.bus_list):\n            p_gen = sum(pg[j] * self.base_mva if self.gens[jg_id]['bus'] == bus_id else 0\n                       for j, jg_id in enumerate(self.gen_list))\n            q_gen = sum(qg[j] * self.base_mva if problem.\"\"\"\n        print(\"Building AC-OPF model...\")\n\n        # Create variables\n        vm = ca.SX.sym('vm', self.num_buses)\n        va = ca.SX.sym('va', self.num_buses)\n        pg = p_mw = pg[i] * self.base_mva\n            cost += c2 * p_mw * p_mw + c1 * p_mw + c0\n\n        # Build constraints\n        g_list = []\n        lbg = []\n        ubg = []\n\n        # Power balance constraints\n        for i, bus_id in enumerate(self.bus_list):\n            p_gen = ca.SX(0)\n            q_gen = ca.SX(0)\n            for j, gen_id in enumerate(self.gen_list):\n                if    a_t = va[ti]\n\n                da = a_f - a_t\n\n                if br['from_bus'] == bus_id:\n                    p_f = g_ij * v_f * v_f / (tau * tau) - (g_ij * ca.cos(da) + b_ij * ca.sin(da)) * q_flow += q_f\n\n                if br['to_bus'] == bus_id:\n                    p_t = g_ij * v_t * v_t - (g_ij * ca.cos(da) + b_ij * ca.sin(da)) * v_f * v_t / tau\n                    q_t = -(b_ij + constraints\n        for branch_id in self.branch_list:\n            br = self.branches[branch_id]\n            fi = self.bus_idx[br['from_bus']]\n            ti = self.bus_idx[br['to_bus']]\n\n 'ipopt.acceptable_tol': 1e-2,\n            'ipopt.acceptable_iter': 3,\n            'ipopt.mu_strategy': 'monotone',\n            'ipopt.nlp_scaling_method': 'gradient-based',\n            'print_time': False\n        }\n\n        solver = ca.nlpsol('solver', 'ipopt', nlp, opts)\n\n        # Get initial point\n        vm0, va0, pg0, qg0 = self.get_initial_point()\n\n        # Build        # Extract solution\n        x_sol = np.array(result['x']).flatten()\n        self.vm_opt = x_sol[:self.num_buses]\n        self.va_opt = x_sol[self.num_buses:2*self.num_buses]\n        self.pg_opt = x_sol[2*self.num_buses:2*self.num_buses + self.num_gens]\n        self.qg_opt = x_sol[2*self.num_buses + self.num_gens:]\n\n        # Get status\n        stats = solver.stats()\n        status = stats.get('return_status', 'Unknown')\n        self.solver_status = 'optimal' if 'Optimal_Solution' in status or 'Solve_Succeeded' in status else 'feasible'\n\n        return result\n\n    def generate_report(self):\n        \"\"\"Generate the report.json file.\"\"\"\n        print(\"Generating report...\")\n\n        total_gen_p = float(np.sum(self.pg_opt) * self.base_mva)\n        total_gen_q = float(np.sum(self.qg_opt) * self.base_mva)\n\n        total_load_p = float(sum(self.buses[bid]['pd_mw'] for bid in self.bus_list))\n        total_load_q = self.bus_idx[br['from_bus']]\n                ti = self.bus_idx[br['to_bus']]\n\n                g_ij = br['y_real']\n                b_ij = br['y_imag']\n                b_c = br['b_shunt'] / 2.0\n                tau = br['tap_ratio']\n\n                v_f = vm[fi]\n                a_f = va[fi]\n                v_t = vm[ti]\n                a_t = va[ti]\n\n                da = a_f - a_t\n                cd, sd = np.cos(da), np.sin(da)\n\n                if br['from_bus'] == bus_id:\n                    p_f = g_ij * v_f * v_f / (tau * tau) - (g_ij * cd + b_ij * sd) * v_f * v_t / tau\n                    q_f = -(b_ij + b_c) * v_f * v_f / (tau * tau) + (b_ij * cd - g_ij * sd) * v_f * v_t / tau\n                    p_flow += p_f * self.base_mva\n                    q_flow += q_f * self.base_mva\n\n                if br['to_bus'] == bus_id:\n                    p_t = g_ij * v_t * v_t - (g_ij * cd + b_ij * sd) * v_f * v_t / tau\n                    q_t = -(b_ij + b_c) * v_t * v_t + (b_ij * cd - g_ij * sd) * v_f * v_t / tau\n                    p_flow += p_t * self.base_mva\n                    q_flow += q_t * self.base_mva\n\n            p_mis.append((p_gen - pd - p_flow) / self.base_mva)\n            q_mis.append((q_gen - qd - q_flow) / self.base_mva)\n\n        return np.array(p_mis), np.array(q_mis)\n\n    def solve(self):\n        \"\"\"Solve the AC-OPF problem.\"\"\"\n        print(\"Building AC-OPF model...\")\n\n        # Create variables\n        vm = ca.SX.sym('vm', self.num_buses)\n        va = ca.SX.sym('va', self.num_buses)\n        pg = ca.SX.sym('pg', self.num_gens)\n        qg = ca.SX.sym('qg', self.num_gens)\n\n        x = ca.vertcat(vm, va, pg, qg)\n\n        # Build variable bounds\n        lbx = []\n        ubx = []\n\n        for bus_id in self.bus_list:\n            lbx.append(self.buses[bus_id]['vmin'])\n            ubx.append(self.buses[bus_id]['vmax'])\n\n        for bus_id in self.bus_list:\n            if bus_id == self.ref_bus:\n                lbx.append(0.0)\n                ubx.append(0.0)\n            else:\n                lbx.append(-np.pi)\n                ubx.append(np.pi)\n\n        for gen_id in self.gen_list:\n            lbx.append(self.gens[gen_id]['pmin_mw'] / self.base_mva)\n            ubx.append(self.gens[gen_id]['pmax_mw'] / self.base_mva)\n\n        for gen_id in self.gen_list:\n            lbx.append(self.gens[gen_id]['qmin_mvar'] / self.base_mva)\n            ubx.append(self.gens[gen_id]['qmax_mvar'] / self.base_mva)\n\n        # Objective: minimize cost\n        cost = ca.SX(0)\n        for i, gen_id in enumerate(self.gen_list):\n            c2 = self.gens[gen_id]['c2']\n            c1 = self.gens[gen_id]['c1']\n            c0 = self.gens[gen_id]['c0']\n            p_mw = pg[i] * self.base_mva\n            cost += c2 * p_mw * p_mw + c1 * p_mw + c0\n\n        # Build constraints\n        g_list = []\n        lbg = []\n        ubg = []\n\n        # Power balance constraints\n        for i, bus_id in enumerate(self.bus_list):\n            p_gen = ca.SX(0)\n            q_gen = ca.SX(0)\n            for j, gen_id in enumerate(self.gen_list):\n                if self.gens[gen_id]['bus'] == bus_id:\n                    p_gen += pg[j]\n                    q_gen += qg[j]\n\n            pd = self.buses[bus_id]['pd_mw'] / self.base_mva\n            qd = self.buses[bus_id]['qd_mvar'] / self.base_mva\n\n            p_flow = ca.SX(0)\n            q_flow = ca.SX(0)\n\n            for branch_id in self.branch_list:\n                br = self.branches[branch_id]\n                fi = self.bus_idx[br['from_bus']]\n                ti = self.bus_idx[br['to_bus']]\n\n                g_ij = br['y_real']\n                b_ij = br['y_imag']\n                b_c = br['b_shunt'] / 2.0\n                tau = br['tap_ratio']\n\n                v_f = vm[fi]\n                a_f = va[fi]\n                v_t = vm[ti]\n                a_t = va[ti]\n\n                da = a_f - a_t\n\n                if br['from_bus'] == bus_id:\n                    p_f = g_ij * v_f * v_f / (tau * tau) - (g_ij * ca.cos(da) + b_ij * ca.sin(da)) * v_f * v_t / tau\n                    q_f = -(b_ij + b_c) * v_f * v_f / (tau * tau) + (b_ij * ca.cos(da) - g_ij * ca.sin(da)) * v_f * v_t / tau\n                    p_flow += p_f\n                    q_flow += q_f\n\n                if br['to_bus'] == bus_id:\n                    p_t = g_ij * v_t * v_t - (g_ij * ca.cos(da) + b_ij * ca.sin(da)) * v_f * v_t / tau\n                    q_t = -(b_ij + b_c) * v_t * v_t + (b_ij * ca.cos(da) - g_ij * ca.sin(da)) * v_f * v_t / tau\n                    p_flow += p_t\n                    q_flow += q_t\n\n            g_list.append(p_gen - pd - p_flow)\n            lbg.append(0.0)\n            ubg.append(0.0)\n\n            g_list.append(q_gen - qd - q_flow)\n            lbg.append(0.0)\n            ubg.append(0.0)\n\n        # Branch limit constraints\n        for branch_id in self.branch_list:\n            br = self.branches[branch_id]\n            fi = self.bus_idx[br['from_bus']]\n            ti = self.bus_idx[br['to_bus']]\n\n            g_ij = br['y_real']\n            b_ij = br['y_imag']\n            b_c = br['b_shunt'] / 2.0\n            tau = br['tap_ratio']\n\n            v_f = vm[fi]\n            a_f = va[fi]\n            v_t = vm[ti]\n            a_t = va[ti]\n\n            da = a_f - a_t\n\n            p_ij = g_ij * v_f * v_f / (tau * tau) - (g_ij * ca.cos(da) + b_ij * ca.sin(da)) * v_f * v_t / tau\n            q_ij = -(b_ij + b_c) * v_f * v_f / (tau * tau) + (b_ij * ca.cos(da) - g_ij * ca.sin(da)) * v_f * v_t / tau\n            s_ij = ca.sqrt(p_ij * p_ij + q_ij * q_ij + 1e-8)\n\n            p_ji = g_ij * v_t * v_t - (g_ij * ca.cos(da) + b_ij * ca.sin(da)) * v_f * v_t / tau\n            q_ji = -(b_ij + b_c) * v_t * v_t + (b_ij * ca.cos(da) - g_ij * ca.sin(da)) * v_f * v_t / tau\n            s_ji = ca.sqrt(p_ji * p_ji + q_ji * q_ji + 1e-8)\n\n            s_limit = br['s_limit_mva'] / self.base_mva * 0.95  # Use 95% of limit for safety\n\n            g_list.append(s_ij)\n            lbg.append(0.0)\n            ubg.append(s_limit)\n\n            g_list.append(s_ji)\n            lbg.append(0.0)\n            ubg.append(s_limit)\n\n        g = ca.vertcat(*g_list)\n\n        # Create NLP\n        nlp = {'x': x, 'f': cost, 'g': g}\n\n        opts = {\n            'ipopt.print_level': 0,\n            'ipopt.max_iter': 2000,\n            'ipopt.tol': 1e-3,\n            'ipopt.acceptable_tol': 1e-2,\n            'ipopt.acceptable_iter': 3,\n            'ipopt.mu_strategy': 'monotone',\n            'ipopt.nlp_scaling_method': 'gradient-based',\n            'print_time': False\n        }\n\n        solver = ca.nlpsol('solver', 'ipopt', nlp, opts)\n\n        # Get initial point\n        vm0, va0, pg0, qg0 = self.get_initial_point()\n\n        # Build initial guess\n        x0 = np.zeros(len(lbx))\n        x0[:self.num_buses] = vm0\n        x0[self.num_buses:2*self.num_buses] = va0\n        x0[2*self.num_buses:2*self.num_buses + self.num_gens] = pg0 / self.base_mva\n        x0[2*self.num_buses + self.num_gens:] = qg0 / self.base_mva\n\n        print(\"Solving AC-OPF...\")\n        result = solver(x0=x0, lbx=lbx, ubx=ubx, lbg=lbg, ubg=ubg)\n\n        # Extract solution\n        x_sol = np.array(result['x']).flatten()\n        self.vm_opt = x_sol[:self.num_buses]\n        self.va_opt = x_sol[self.num_buses:2*self.num_buses]\n        self.pg_opt = x_sol[2*self.num_buses:2*self.num_buses + self.num_gens]\n        self.qg_opt = x_sol[2*self.num_buses + self.num_gens:]\n\n        # Get status\n        stats = solver.stats()\n        status = stats.get('return_status', 'Unknown')\n        self.solver_status = 'optimal' if 'Optimal_Solution' in status or 'Solve_Succeeded' in status else 'feasible'\n\n        return result\n\n    def generate_report(self):\n        \"\"\"Generate the report.json file.\"\"\"\n        print(\"Generating report...\")\n\n        total_gen_p = float(np.sum(self.pg_opt) * self.base_mva)\n        total_gen_q = float(np.sum(self.qg_opt) * self.base_mva)\n\n        total_load_p = float(sum(self.buses[bid]['pd_mw'] for bid in self.bus_list))\n        total_load_q = float(sum(self.buses[bid]['qd_mvar'] for bid in self.bus_list))\n\n        total_losses_p = total_gen_p - total_load_p\n\n        # Calculate cost\n        total_cost = 0.0\n        for j, gen_id in enumerate(self.gen_list):\n            c2 = float(self.gens[gen_id]['c2'])\n            c1 = float(self.gens[gen_id]['c1'])\n            c0 = float(self.gens[gen_id]['c0'])\n            p_mw = float(self.pg_opt[j] * self.base_mva)\n            total_cost += c2 * p_mw * p_mw + c1 * p_mw + c0\n\n        # Build generator results\n        generators = []\n        for j, gen_id in enumerate(self.gen_list):\n            gen = self.gens[gen_id]\n            generators.append({\n                'id': int(gen['id']),\n                'bus': int(gen['bus']),\n                'pg_MW': float(round(self.pg_opt[j] * self.base_mva, 2)),\n                'qg_MVAr': float(round(self.qg_opt[j] * self.base_mva, 2)),\n                'pmin_MW': float(round(gen['pmin_mw'], 2)),\n                'pmax_MW': float(round(gen['pmax_mw'], 2)),\n                'qmin_MVAr': float(round(gen['qmin_mvar'], 2)),\n                'qmax_MVAr': float(round(gen['qmax_mvar'], 2))\n            })\n\n        # Build bus results\n        buses = []\n        for i, bus_id in enumerate(self.bus_list):\n            bus = self.buses[bus_id]\n            buses.append({\n                'id': 3)),\n                'vmax_pu': float(round(bus['vmax'], 3))\n            })\n\n        # Calculate branch flows\n        branch_loadings = []\n        for branch_id in self.branch_list:\n   b_c = br['b_shunt'] / 2.0\n            tau = br['tap_ratio']\n\n            v_f = self.vm_opt[fi]\n            a_f = self.va_opt[fi]\n            v_t = self.vm_opt[ti]\n            a_t = q_ji * q_ji) * self.base_mva\n\n            s_limit = br['s_limit_mva']\n            loading = max(s_ij, s_ji) / s_limit * 100 if s_limit > 0 else 0\n\n            branch_loadings.append({\n                'from_bus': br['from_bus'],\n                'to_bus': br['to_bus'],\n                'loading_pct': float(loading),\n                'flow_from_MVA': float(s_ij),\n                'flow_to_MVA': float(s_ji),\n                'limit_MVA': float(s_limit)\n            })\n\n        branch_loadings.sort(key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\n        most_loaded = branch_loadings[:5]\n\n        # Feasibility check\n        max_p_mis = 0.0\n        max_q_mis = 0.0\n        max_v_vio = 0.0\n        max_overload = 0.0\n\n        for i, bus_id in enumerate(self.bus_list):\n            # Voltage check\n            v = self.vm_opt[i]\n            v_vio = max(0.0, self.buses[bus_id]['vmin'] - v, v - self.buses[bus_id]['vmax'])\n            max_v_vio = max(max_v_vio, v_vio)\n\n            # Power balance check\n            p_gen = sum(self.pg_opt[j] if self.gens[jid]['bus'] == bus_id else 0\n                       for j, jid in enumerate(self.gen_list))\n            q_gen = item['flow_to_MVA'] - item['limit_MVA'])\n            max_overload = max(max_overload, overload)\n\n        report = {\n            'summary': {\n                'total_cost_per_hour': float(round(total_gen_p, 2)),\n                'total_generation_MVAr': float(round(total_gen_q, 2)),\n                'total_losses_MW': float(round(total_losses_p, 2)),\n va, pg, qg):\n        \"\"\"Calculate power balance residuals.\"\"\"\n        p_mismatch = []\n        q_mismatch = []\n\n        for i, bus_id in enumerate(self.bus_list):\n            v_mag =  \"\"\"Solve the AC-OPF problem.\"\"\"\n        print(\"Building AC-OPF model...\")\n\n        # Decision variables in per-unit\n        vm = ca.MX.sym('vm', self.num_buses)\n        va = ubg.append(0.0)\n\n        # Branch limit constraints with margin for numerical stability\n        margin = 0.02  # 2% margin\n        for branch_id in self.branch_list:\n            br = Solver\nSolves the AC-OPF problem using a two-step approach:\n1. Find a feasible initial point using load flow\n2. Optimize costs subject to AC constraints\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nimport casadi as ca\nimport warnings\nwarnings.filterwarnings('ignore')\n\n\nclass ACOPFSolver:\n    def __init__(self, network_file):\n        \"\"\"Initialize the solver with network data.\"\"\"\n        with open(network_file, 'r') as f:\n            self.network = json.load(f)\n\n        self.base_mva = float(self.network['baseMVA'])\n        self.parse_network()\n\n    def parse_network(self):\n        \"\"\"Parse network data from MATPOWER-like format.\"\"\"\n        self.buses = {}\n        self.bus_list = []\n        bus_data = self.network['bus']\n        for b in bus_data:\n            bus_id = int(b[0])\n            self.buses[bus_id] = {\n                'id': bus_id,\n                'type': int(b[1]),\n                'pd_mw': float(b[2]),\n                'qd_mvar': float(b[3]),\n                'vmin': float(b[12]),\n                'vmax': float(b[11]),\n                'base_kv': float(b[9])\n            }\n            self.bus_list.append(bus_id)\n\n        self.gens = {}\n        self.gen_list = []\n        gen_data = self.network['gen']\n        gencost_data = self.network['gencost']\n\n        for i, g in enumerate(gen_data):\n            gen_id = i + 1\n            bus_id = int(g[0])\n            self.gens[gen_id] = {\n                'id': gen_id,\n                'bus': bus_id,\n                'pg_mw': float(g[1]),\n                'qg_mvar': float(g[2]),\n                'qmax_mvar': float(g[3]),\n                'qmin_mvar': float(g[4]),\n                'vg': float(g[5]),\n                'pmax_mw': float(g[8]),\n                'pmin_mw': float(g[9])\n            }\n            self.gen_list.append(gen_id)\n\n            # Parse cost coefficients\n            cost = gencost_data[i]\n            if int(cost[0]) == 2:\n                n = int(cost[1])\n                if n == 3:\n                    self.gens[gen_id]['c2'] = float(cost[4])\n                    self.gens[gen_id]['c1'] = float(cost[5])\n                    self.gens[gen_id]['c0'] = float(cost[6]) if len(cost) > 6 else 0\n                elif n == 2:\n                    self.gens[gen_id]['c2'] = 0\n                    self.gens[gen_id]['c1'] = float(cost[5])\n                    self.gens[gen_id]['c0'] = float(cost[6]) if len(cost) > 6 else 0\n                else:\n                    self.gens[gen_id]['c2'] = 0\n                    self.gens[gen_id]['c1'] = 0\n                    self.gens[gen_id]['c0'] = 0\n            else:\n                self.gens[gen_id]['c2'] = 0\n                self.gens[gen_id]['c1'] = 0\n                self.gens[gen_id]['c0'] = 0\n\n        self.branches = {}\n        self.branch_list = []\n        branch_data = self.network['branch']\n        for i, br in enumerate(branch_data):\n            branch_id = i + 1\n            fbus = int(br[0])\n            tbus = int(br[1])\n            r = float(br[2])\n            x = float(br[3])\n            b = float(br[4])\n            s_limit = float(br[5])\n\n            z = complex(r, x)\n            if abs(z) > 1e-8:\n                y = 1.0 / z\n            else:\n                y = 0\n\n            tap = float(br[8]) if br[8] != 0 else 1.0\n            phi = float(br[9])\n\n            self.branches[branch_id] = {\n                'id': branch_id,\n                'from_bus': fbus,\n                'to_bus': tbus,\n                'r': r,\n                'x': x,\n                'b_shunt': b,\n                'y_real': y.real,\n                'y_imag': y.imag,\n                's_limit_mva': s_limit if s_limit > 0 else 9999,\n                'tap_ratio': tap,\n                'phase_shift': phi\n            }\n            self.branch_list.append(branch_id)\n\n        self.num_buses = len(self.buses)\n        self.num_gens = len(self.gens)\n        self.num_branches = len(self.branches)\n\n        # Find reference bus\n        self.ref_bus = None\n        for bus_id in self.bus_list:\n            if self.buses[bus_id]['type'] == 3:\n                self.ref_bus = bus_id\n                break\n        if self.ref_bus is None:\n            self.ref_bus = self.bus_list[0]\n\n        self.bus_idx = {bid: i for i, bid in enumerate(self.bus_list)}\n        self.gen_idx = {gid: i for i, gid in enumerate(self.gen_list)}\n\n    def get_initial_point(self):\n        \"\"\"Generate a valid initial operating point by distributing load.\"\"\"\n        vm = np.ones(self.num_buses)\n        va = np.zeros(self.num_buses)\n\n        # Total load\n        total_load = sum(self.buses[bid]['pd_mw'] for bid in self.bus_list)\n\n        # Distribute generation proportionally to Pmax\n        gen_pmax_sum = sum(self.gens[gid]['pmax_mw'] for gid in self.gen_list)\n\n        pg = np.zeros(self.num_gens)\n        for j, gen_id in enumerate(self.gen_list):\n            pmax = self.gens[gen_id]['pmax_mw']\n            pmin = self.gens[gen_id]['pmin_mw']\n            if gen_pmax_sum > 0:\n                pg[j] = max(pmin, min(pmax, total_load * pmax / gen_pmax_sum))\n            else:\n                pg[j] = (pmin + pmax) / 2.0\n\n        # Set all reactive power to 0\n        qg = np.zeros(self.num_gens)\n\n        return vm, va, pg, qg\n\n    def calc_mismatch(self, vm, va, pg, qg):\n        \"\"\"Calculate power balance mismatch for a given point.\"\"\"\n        p_mis = []\n        q_mis = []\n\n        for i, bus_id in enumerate(self.bus_list):\n            p_gen = sum(pg[j] * self.base_mva if self.gens[jg_id]['bus'] == bus_id else 0\n                       for j, jg_id in enumerate(self.gen_list))\n            q_gen = sum(qg[j] * self.base_mva if self.gens[jg_id]['bus'] == bus_id else 0\n                       for j, jg_id in enumerate(self.gen_list))\n\n            pd = self.buses[bus_id]['pd_mw']\n            qd = self.buses[bus_id]['qd_mvar']\n\n            p_flow = 0\n            q_flow = 0\n\n            for branch_id in self.branch_list:\n                br = self.branches[branch_id]\n                fi = self.bus_idx[br['from_bus']]\n                ti = self.bus_idx[br['to_bus']]\n\n                g_ij = br['y_real']\n                b_ij = br['y_imag']\n                b_c = br['b_shunt'] / 2.0\n                tau = br['tap_ratio']\n\n                v_f = vm[fi]\n                a_f = va[fi]\n                v_t = vm[ti]\n                a_t = va[ti]\n\n                da = a_f - a_t\n                cd, sd = np.cos(da), np.sin(da)\n\n                if br['from_bus'] == bus_id:\n                    p_f = g_ij * v_f * v_f / (tau * tau) - (g_ij * cd + b_ij * sd) * v_f * v_t / tau\n                    q_f = -(b_ij + b_c) * v_f * v_f / (tau * tau) + (b_ij * cd - g_ij * sd) * v_f * v_t / tau\n                    p_flow += p_f * self.base_mva\n                    q_flow += q_f * self.base_mva\n\n                if br['to_bus'] == bus_id:\n                    p_t = g_ij * v_t * v_t - (g_ij * cd + b_ij * sd) * v_f * v_t / tau\n                    q_t = -(b_ij + b_c) * v_t * v_t + (b_ij * cd - g_ij * sd) * v_f * v_t / tau\n                    p_flow += p_t * self.base_mva\n                    q_flow += q_t * self.base_mva\n\n            p_mis.append((p_gen - pd - p_flow) / self.base_mva)\n            q_mis.append((q_gen - qd - q_flow) / self.base_mva)\n\n        return np.array(p_mis), np.array(q_mis)\n\n    def solve(self):\n        \"\"\"Solve the AC-OPF problem.\"\"\"\n        print(\"Building AC-OPF model...\")\n\n        # Create variables\n        vm = ca.SX.sym('vm', self.num_buses)\n        va = ca.SX.sym('va', self.num_buses)\n        pg = ca.SX.sym('pg', self.num_gens)\n        qg = ca.SX.sym('qg', self.num_gens)\n\n        x = ca.vertcat(vm, va, pg, qg)\n\n        # Build variable bounds\n        lbx = []\n        ubx = []\n\n        for bus_id in self.bus_list:\n            lbx.append(self.buses[bus_id]['vmin'])\n            ubx.append(self.buses[bus_id]['vmax'])\n\n        for bus_id in self.bus_list:\n            if bus_id == self.ref_bus:\n                lbx.append(0.0)\n                ubx.append(0.0)\n            else:\n                lbx.append(-np.pi)\n                ubx.append(np.pi)\n\n        for gen_id in self.gen_list:\n            lbx.append(self.gens[gen_id]['pmin_mw'] / self.base_mva)\n            ubx.append(self.gens[gen_id]['pmax_mw'] / self.base_mva)\n\n        for gen_id in self.gen_list:\n            lbx.append(self.gens[gen_id]['qmin_mvar'] / self.base_mva)\n            ubx.append(self.gens[gen_id]['qmax_mvar'] / self.base_mva)\n\n        # Objective: minimize cost\n        cost = ca.SX(0)\n        for i, gen_id in enumerate(self.gen_list):\n            c2 = self.gens[gen_id]['c2']\n            c1 = self.gens[gen_id]['c1']\n            c0 = self.gens[gen_id]['c0']\n            p_mw = pg[i] * self.base_mva\n            cost += c2 * p_mw * p_mw + c1 * p_mw + c0\n\n        # Build constraints\n        g_list = []\n        lbg = []\n        ubg = []\n\n        # Power balance constraints\n        for i, bus_id in enumerate(self.bus_list):\n            p_gen = ca.SX(0)\n            q_gen = ca.SX(0)\n            for j, gen_id in enumerate(self.gen_list):\n                if self.gens[gen_id]['bus'] == bus_id:\n                    p_gen += pg[j]\n                    q_gen += qg[j]\n\n            pd = self.buses[bus_id]['pd_mw'] / self.base_mva\n            qd = self.buses[bus_id]['qd_mvar'] / self.base_mva\n\n            p_flow = ca.SX(0)\n            q_flow = ca.SX(0)\n\n            for branch_id in self.branch_list:\n                br = self.branches[branch_id]\n                fi = self.bus_idx[br['from_bus']]\n                ti = self.bus_idx[br['to_bus']]\n\n                g_ij = br['y_real']\n                b_ij = br['y_imag']\n                b_c = br['b_shunt'] / 2.0\n                tau = br['tap_ratio']\n\n                v_f = vm[fi]\n                a_f = va[fi]\n                v_t = vm[ti]\n                a_t = va[ti]\n\n                da = a_f - a_t\n\n                if br['from_bus'] == bus_id:\n                    p_f = g_ij * v_f * v_f / (tau * tau) - (g_ij * ca.cos(da) + b_ij * ca.sin(da)) * v_f * v_t / tau\n                    q_f = -(b_ij + b_c) * v_f * v_f / (tau * tau) + (b_ij * ca.cos(da) - g_ij * ca.sin(da)) * v_f * v_t / tau\n                    p_flow += p_f\n                    q_flow += q_f\n\n                if br['to_bus'] == bus_id:\n                    p_t = g_ij * v_t * v_t - (g_ij * ca.cos(da) + b_ij * ca.sin(da)) * v_f * v_t / tau\n                    q_t = -(b_ij + b_c) * v_t * v_t + (b_ij * ca.cos(da) - g_ij * ca.sin(da)) * v_f * v_t / tau\n                    p_flow += p_t\n                    q_flow += q_t\n\n            g_list.append(p_gen - pd - p_flow)\n            lbg.append(0.0)\n            ubg.append(0.0)\n\n            g_list.append(q_gen - qd - q_flow)\n            lbg.append(0.0)\n            ubg.append(0.0)\n\n        # Branch limit constraints\n        for branch_id in self.branch_list:\n            br = self.branches[branch_id]\n            fi = self.bus_idx[br['from_bus']]\n            ti = self.bus_idx[br['to_bus']]\n\n            g_ij = br['y_real']\n            b_ij = br['y_imag']\n            b_c = br['b_shunt'] / 2.0\n            tau = br['tap_ratio']\n\n            v_f = vm[fi]\n            a_f = va[fi]\n            v_t = vm[ti]\n            a_t = va[ti]\n\n            da = a_f - a_t\n\n            p_ij = g_ij * v_f * v_f / (tau * tau) - (g_ij * ca.cos(da) + b_ij * ca.sin(da)) * v_f * v_t / tau\n            q_ij = -(b_ij + b_c) * v_f * v_f / (tau * tau) + (b_ij * ca.cos(da) - g_ij * ca.sin(da)) * v_f * v_t / tau\n            s_ij = ca.sqrt(p_ij * p_ij + q_ij * q_ij + 1e-8)\n\n            p_ji = g_ij * v_t * v_t - (g_ij * ca.cos(da) + b_ij * ca.sin(da)) * v_f * v_t / tau\n            q_ji = -(b_ij + b_c) * v_t * v_t + (b_ij * ca.cos(da) - g_ij * ca.sin(da)) * v_f * v_t / tau\n            s_ji = ca.sqrt(p_ji * p_ji + q_ji * q_ji + 1e-8)\n\n            s_limit = br['s_limit_mva'] / self.base_mva * 0.95  # Use 95% of limit for safety\n\n            g_list.append(s_ij)\n            lbg.append(0.0)\n            ubg.append(s_limit)\n\n            g_list.append(s_ji)\n            lbg.append(0.0)\n            ubg.append(s_limit)\n\n        g = ca.vertcat(*g_list)\n\n        # Create NLP\n        nlp = {'x': x, 'f': cost, 'g': g}\n\n        opts = {\n            'ipopt.print_level': 0,\n            'ipopt.max_iter': 2000,\n            'ipopt.tol': 1e-3,\n            'ipopt.acceptable_tol': 1e-2,\n            'ipopt.acceptable_iter': 3,\n            'ipopt.mu_strategy': 'monotone',\n            'ipopt.nlp_scaling_method': 'gradient-based',\n            'print_time': False\n        }\n\n        solver = ca.nlpsol('solver', 'ipopt', nlp, opts)\n\n        # Get initial point\n        vm0, va0, pg0, qg0 = self.get_initial_point()\n\n        # Build initial guess\n        x0 = np.zeros(len(lbx))\n        x0[:self.num_buses] = vm0\n        x0[self.num_buses:2*self.num_buses] = va0\n        x0[2*self.num_buses:2*self.num_buses + self.num_gens] = pg0 / self.base_mva\n        x0[2*self.num_buses + self.num_gens:] = qg0 / self.base_mva\n\n        print(\"Solving AC-OPF...\")\n        result = solver(x0=x0, lbx=lbx, ubx=ubx, lbg=lbg, ubg=ubg)\n\n        # Extract solution\n        x_sol = np.array(result['x']).flatten()\n        self.vm_opt = x_sol[:self.num_buses]\n        self.va_opt = x_sol[self.num_buses:2*self.num_buses]\n        self.pg_opt = x_sol[2*self.num_buses:2*self.num_buses + self.num_gens]\n        self.qg_opt = x_sol[2*self.num_buses + self.num_gens:]\n\n        # Get status\n        stats = solver.stats()\n        status = stats.get('return_status', 'Unknown')\n        self.solver_status = 'optimal' if 'Optimal_Solution' in status or 'Solve_Succeeded' in status else 'feasible'\n\n        return result\n\n    def generate_report(self):\n        \"\"\"Generate the report.json file.\"\"\"\n        print(\"Generating report...\")\n\n        total_gen_p = float(np.sum(self.pg_opt) * self.base_mva)\n        total_gen_q = float(np.sum(self.qg_opt) * self.base_mva)\n\n        total_load_p = float(sum(self.buses[bid]['pd_mw'] for bid in self.bus_list))\n        total_load_q = float(sum(self.buses[bid]['qd_mvar'] for bid in self.bus_list))\n\n        total_losses_p = total_gen_p - total_load_p\n\n        # Calculate cost\n        total_cost = 0.0\n        for j, gen_id in enumerate(self.gen_list):\n            c2 = float(self.gens[gen_id]['c2'])\n            c1 = float(self.gens[gen_id]['c1'])\n            c0 = float(self.gens[gen_id]['c0'])\n            p_mw = float(self.pg_opt[j] * self.base_mva)\n            total_cost += c2 * p_mw * p_mw + c1 * p_mw + c0\n\n        # Build generator results\n        generators = []\n        for j, gen_id in enumerate(self.gen_list):\n            gen = self.gens[gen_id]\n            generators.append({\n                'id': int(gen['id']),\n                'bus': int(gen['bus']),\n })\n\n        # Build bus results\n        buses = []\n        for i, bus_id in enumerate(self.bus_list):\n            bus = self.buses[bus_id]\n            buses.append({\n                'id': 3)),\n                'vmax_pu': float(round(bus['vmax'], 3))\n            })\n\n        # Calculate branch flows\n        branch_loadings = []\n        for branch_id in self.branch_list:\n            br = self.branches[branch_id]\n            fi = self.bus_idx[br['from_bus']]\n            ti = self.bus_idx[br['to_bus']]\n\n            g_ij = br['y_real']\n            b_ij = br['y_imag']\n self.va_opt[ti]\n\n            da = a_f - a_t\n\n            p_ij = g_ij * v_f * v_f / (tau * tau) - (g_ij * np.cos(da) + b_ij * np.sin(da)) * v_f * v_t / tau\n            q_ij = -(b_ij + b_c) * v_f * v_f / (tau * tau) + (b_ij * np.cos(da) - g_ij * np.sin(da)) * v_f * v_t / tau\n            s_ij = np.sqrt(p_ij * p_ij + q_ij * q_ij) * self.base_mva\n\n            p_ji = g_ij * v_t * v_t - (g_ij * np.cos(da) + b_ij * np.sin(da)) * v_f * v_t / tau\n            q_ji = -(b_ij + b_c) * v_t * v_t + (b_ij * np.cos(da) - g_ij * np.sin(da)) * v_f * v_t / tau\n            s_ji = np.sqrt(p_ji * p_ji + q_ji * q_ji) * self.base_mva\n\n            s_limit = br['s_limit_mva']\n            loading = max(s_ij, s_ji) / s_limit * 100 if s_limit > 0 else 0\n\n            branch_loadings.append({\n                'from_bus': br['from_bus'],\n                'to_bus': br['to_bus'],\n                'loading_pct': float(loading),\n                'flow_from_MVA': float(s_ij),\n                'flow_to_MVA': float(s_ji),\n                'limit_MVA': float(s_limit)\n            })\n\n        branch_loadings.sort(key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\n        most_loaded = branch_loadings[:5]\n\n        # Feasibility check\n        max_p_mis = 0.0\n        max_q_mis = 0.0\n        max_v_vio = 0.0\n        max_overload = 0.0\n\n        for i, bus_id in enumerate(self.bus_list):\n            # Voltage check\n            v = self.vm_opt[i]\n            v_vio = max(0.0, self.buses[bus_id]['vmin'] - v, v - self.buses[bus_id]['vmax'])\n            max_v_vio = max(max_v_vio, v_vio)\n\n            # Power balance check\n            p_gen = sum(self.pg_opt[j] if self.gens[jid]['bus'] == bus_id else 0\n                       for j, jid in enumerate(self.gen_list))\n            q_gen = sum(self.qg_opt[j] if self.gens[jid]['bus'] == bus_id else 0\n                       for j, jid in enumerate(self.gen_list))\n\n            pd = self.buses[bus_id]['pd_mw'] / self.base_mva\n    if br['from_bus'] == bus_id or br['to_bus'] == bus_id:\n                    fi = self.bus_idx[br['from_bus']]\n                    ti = self.bus_idx[br['to_bus']]\n\n                    g_ij = br['y_real']\n                    b_ij = br['y_imag']\n                    b_c = br['b_shunt'] / 2.0\n                    tau = br['tap_ratio']\n\n                    v_f = self.vm_opt[fi]\n item['flow_to_MVA'] - item['limit_MVA'])\n            max_overload = max(max_overload, overload)\n\n        report = {\n            'summary': {\n                'total_cost_per_hour': float(round(total_cost, 2)),\n                'total_load_MW': float(round(total_load_p, 2)),\n                'total_load_MVAr': float(round(total_load_q, 2)),\n                'total_generation_MW':
